**[00:36:07.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000260)  
yung mga reforma sa

**[00:36:09.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000261)  
Expanded National Nutrition Program

**[00:36:11.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000262)  
na kung saan nais po natin

**[00:36:13.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000263)  
mabigyan ng pagkain universally

**[00:36:15.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000264)  
ang K to Grade 3.

**[00:36:17.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000265)  
This has been a

**[00:36:19.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000266)  
focus area of so many of

**[00:36:21.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000267)  
the persons here, especially

**[00:36:23.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000268)  
Sen. Cayetano has been

**[00:36:25.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000269)  
focused on fighting stunting in our

**[00:36:27.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000270)  
country. Ito pong National

**[00:36:29.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000271)  
Nutrition Program na ito, yung

**[00:36:31.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000272)  
libring pagkain ay isang napakalaking

**[00:36:33.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000273)  
reforma at kailangan po natin

**[00:36:35.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000274)  
itong mapondohan.

**[00:36:37.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000275)  
Uulitin ko po, for

**[00:36:39.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000276)  
2026, ginawang universal

**[00:36:41.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000277)  
ang Kinder at Grade 1

**[00:36:43.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000278)  
at about 22 to

**[00:36:45.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000279)  
25 pesos per meal.

**[00:36:47.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000280)  
Tinaas po yung budget

**[00:36:49.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000281)  
to 27 billion. Ang lumabas

**[00:36:51.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000282)  
po sa GAB

**[00:36:53.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000283)  
ay nasa 11, less than

**[00:36:55.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000284)  
12 billion pesos. Ang laki ng slash.

**[00:36:57.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000285)  
Ang laki po nang binawas po

**[00:36:59.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000286)  
dito. And yet,

**[00:37:01.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000287)  
when we pass the law, hindi lang po

**[00:37:03.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000288)  
Kinder at Grade 1 yung nais natin mabigyan

**[00:37:05.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000289)  
ng pagkain hanggang Grade 3 na.

**[00:37:07.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000290)  
So you can imagine ano po yung

**[00:37:09.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000291)  
laki ng budget na hahabulin po natin

**[00:37:11.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000292)  
kung nais nating makita

**[00:37:13.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000293)  
na ang Kinder, Grade 1,

**[00:37:15.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000294)  
Grade 2 at Grade 3 sa lahat

**[00:37:17.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000295)  
ng public schools po natin

**[00:37:19.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000296)  
ay may nakukuhang pagkain.

**[00:37:21.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000297)  
Alam ko rin po na mula sa 25

**[00:37:23.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000298)  
pesos, kinailangan na rin pong

**[00:37:25.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000299)  
itaas to 35 pesos per

**[00:37:27.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000300)  
meal. Yun po yung suwisyon

**[00:37:29.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000301)  
ng DepEd. Tama naman po ano na yung

**[00:37:31.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000302)  
mabibili natin sa presyo ng

**[00:37:33.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000303)  
bilihin ngayon sa 25 pesos.

**[00:37:35.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000304)  
Tama lang po na maitaas po yan

**[00:37:37.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000305)  
pero ibig sabihin po, pag tinataas

**[00:37:39.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000306)  
yung per unit price, e baka bungaba

**[00:37:41.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000307)  
naman po yung dami na mga batang

**[00:37:43.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000308)  
makakakuha ng tamang

**[00:37:45.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000309)  
pagkain. So ito po, isa ring

**[00:37:47.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000310)  
focus area na nais po nating makita

**[00:37:49.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000311)  
at nais po nating malaman kung ano yung

**[00:37:51.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000312)  
magiging plano po natin.

**[00:37:53.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000313)  
para po dito.

**[00:37:55.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000314)  
Yung pangatlong focus area po ng inyong

**[00:37:57.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000315)  
komite, yung School Safety Act.

**[00:37:59.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000316)  
Dito po sa Senado, pasado na po

**[00:38:01.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000317)  
ito. Hindi pa rin po ito

**[00:38:03.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000318)  
pasado sa Kongreso pero na meron

**[00:38:05.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000319)  
na po tayong commitment mula po sa ating

**[00:38:07.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000320)  
congressional counterparts

**[00:38:09.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000321)  
to also accelerate the passage of the

**[00:38:11.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000322)  
School Safety Act. Alam po natin

**[00:38:13.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000323)  
na nagkaroon tayo ng

**[00:38:15.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000324)  
mga school shooting at patuloy

**[00:38:17.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000325)  
ang mga kwento ng

**[00:38:19.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000326)  
mga pagbabanta

**[00:38:21.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000327)  
o mga posibleng karahasan sa ating

**[00:38:23.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000328)  
mga eskwelaan. Nais po natin

**[00:38:25.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000329)  
malaman ano yung gagawin po natin

**[00:38:27.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000330)  
dito. Hindi lang naman ito tungkol sa

**[00:38:29.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000331)  
kagamitan. Hindi lang naman po ito tungkol

**[00:38:31.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000332)  
lamang sa mga CCTV

**[00:38:33.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000333)  
at mga metal detectors.

**[00:38:35.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000334)  
The School Safety Act is much more

**[00:38:37.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000335)  
than that. It's a framework

**[00:38:39.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000336)  
to really support school safety in our country.

**[00:38:41.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000337)  
Pero alam po natin na

**[00:38:43.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000338)  
meron pa rin cost ito.

**[00:38:45.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000339)  
Nais po natin makita kung ito

**[00:38:47.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000340)  
po ay nakapondo na po ng maayos

**[00:38:49.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000341)  
for next year. So ito

**[00:38:51.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000342)  
pong tatlo ang focus area

**[00:38:53.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000343)  
ng inyong chairperson.

**[00:38:55.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000344)  
Bawat po isa sa amin

**[00:38:57.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000345)  
dito may iba't iba rin mga focus area.

**[00:38:59.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000346)  
So nais po natin malaman

**[00:39:01.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000347)  
kung ito po ay sapat na

**[00:39:03.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000348)  
mapapondohan po natin. Let me

**[00:39:05.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000349)  
say off the top of

**[00:39:07.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000350)  
this hearing, Secretary

**[00:39:09.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000351)  
Angara, that I'm

**[00:39:11.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000352)  
hoping that we can get back

**[00:39:13.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000353)  
to 4% of GDP

**[00:39:15.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000354)  
for education

**[00:39:17.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000355)  
as a whole. Nais po po

**[00:39:19.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000356)  
na makita na maibalik po

**[00:39:21.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000357)  
natin ang pagpondo sa edukasyon

**[00:39:23.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000358)  
na umaabot sa 4%

**[00:39:25.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000359)  
of GDP. Sa aming pong

**[00:39:27.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000360)  
computation ngayon, short po tayo.

**[00:39:29.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000361)  
So with the support

**[00:39:31.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000362)  
of the chairman, Senator JV

**[00:39:33.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000363)  
and of course our Senate President,

**[00:39:35.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000364)  
even our Minority Floor Leader, Senator Allan who is here,

**[00:39:37.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000365)  
nais po natin makita na

**[00:39:39.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000366)  
mapondohan ng maayos ang edukasyon sa ating

**[00:39:41.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000367)  
bansa. Lagi po pong

**[00:39:43.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000368)  
sinasabi na ang

**[00:39:45.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000369)  
edukasyon po ay hindi po

**[00:39:47.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000370)  
dapat napupulitika. Ang edukasyon ay para sa lahat ng Pilipino, para sa lahat ng batang Pilipino.

**[00:39:52.820] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000371)  
And much like our measures in the past, alam ko po na kung tayo po yung magsasama-sama at magtutulungan,

**[00:39:59.780] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000372)  
maaabot po natin ang mga hangarin natin ng komiting ito.

**[00:40:03.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000373)  
So maraming salamat, Secretary, and to the DepEd family, and we're looking forward to your presentation.

**[00:40:09.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000374)  
So, does the Senate President have an opening statement?

**[00:40:12.480] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000375)  
Or we can ask the Chairman of Finance first?

**[00:40:18.780] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000376)  
Yeah, sige. So may we ask the Chairman of Finance for his opening statement?

**[00:40:23.700] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000377)  
We'd like to acknowledge Senate President Gatchalian and Senator Pangilinan as well.

**[00:40:29.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000378)  
And Senator Pia Quetano as well.

**[00:40:32.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000379)  
Thank you sa ating pong Vice Chair ng Committee on Finance, Senator Bam Aquino.

**[00:40:37.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000380)  
Magandang araw po sa lahat dito sa ating makasamahan, to my colleagues,

**[00:40:42.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000381)  
to our Senate President, to our Minority Leader, and to my colleagues.

**[00:40:47.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000382)  
Muli, isang magandang araw po sa inyong lahat, especially to the DepEd family.

**[00:40:52.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000383)  
To my colleague, to my schoolmate, and seatmate, former seatmate, Secretary Sani Angara.

**[00:41:00.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000384)  
Magandang umaga po sa inyong lahat.

**[00:41:02.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000385)  
When we began our deliberations of the proposed 2027 National Budget, I called for return to basics,

**[00:41:08.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000386)  
which means giving priority to the services that our people need most,

**[00:41:12.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000387)  
and ensuring that every peso we allocate delivers meaningful results.

**[00:41:17.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000388)  
Sa edukasyon, malinaw ang ibig sabihin ng back to basics.

**[00:41:20.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000389)  
May ligtas bang silid na paaralan?

**[00:41:23.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000390)  
Meron ang ating maanak? May sapat bang guro, aklat at kagamitan?

**[00:41:27.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000391)  
Nakakapasok ba silang busog at handang matuto?

**[00:41:31.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000392)  
At higit sa lahat, natututo ba silang bumasa, umunawa, at magbilang?

**[00:41:36.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000393)  
These are the basic questions that must guide our consideration of the Department of Education's proposed budget.

**[00:41:42.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000395)  
Para sa isang pamilyang nagsisikap maitawid ng araw-araw, malaking pag-asa ang edukasyon.

**[00:41:49.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000396)  
Umaasa ang mga magulang na sa bawat araw na mapapasok ang kaling anak na daragdagan ang kaalaman at kakayahan nitong bumuo sa mas magandang kinabukasan.

**[00:41:59.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000397)  
Our deliberations must therefore examine both the adequacy of funding and the Department's capacity to deliver.

**[00:42:05.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000398)  
We need clear answers on classroom construction, the timely delivery of textbooks and learning materials,

**[00:42:12.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000399)  
and the feeling of teaching vacancies.

**[00:42:16.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000400)  
We also need to understand how learning recovery interventions will reach the children who need them the most.

**[00:42:22.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000401)  
But we should also recognize the progress that has been made.

**[00:42:26.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000402)  
The Philippines' improved performance in the 2025 Program for International Student Assessment, or PISA,

**[00:42:34.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000403)  
with gains in reading, mathematics, and science is an encouraging development.

**[00:42:38.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000404)  
Saludo tayo sa Department of Education.

**[00:42:41.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000405)  
Siyempre sa atin.

**[00:42:42.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000406)  
Sa ating Secretary Angara, sa ating maguro at school leaders, at sa ating mga learners dahil sa tagumpay na ito.

**[00:42:49.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000407)  
Sa kabila ng mga hamon sa ating sistema na edukasyon, may pag-usad tayong nakikita.

**[00:42:55.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000408)  
Ngayon pa man, maraming pa tayong kailangan gawin at pagbutihin.

**[00:42:58.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000409)  
In the past few days, since the Senate received the General Appropriations Bill,

**[00:43:02.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000410)  
we've observed that the House version introduces steep adjustments to the proposed budget,

**[00:43:09.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000411)  
including reductions in computerization,

**[00:43:12.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000412)  
basic education facilities, teacher development, and foreign-assisted projects.

**[00:43:18.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000413)  
This morning, that's what we want to find out.

**[00:43:20.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000414)  
Kaya nga tayo nagkaroon ng budgeteering.

**[00:43:22.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000415)  
Of course, we would like to hear from Secretary Angara and the Department

**[00:43:27.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000416)  
ano po ang magiging epekto nitong mga pagbawas na ito sa ating programa, main program, sa ating DepEd.

**[00:43:37.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000417)  
Kaya nga po, kung kinakailangan talaga, ay gagawan nating paraan.

**[00:43:42.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000418)  
nung isang taon, we are quite proud that in spite of the floodgate scandal,

**[00:43:49.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000419)  
the reforms that were started here in the Senate by then Finance Chair,

**[00:43:53.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000420)  
Senator Gatchal Yan, gave focus and priority to the Department of Education.

**[00:43:59.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000421)  
Kaya nga, ang ganda ho, tignan, that DepEd was at the center, talagang pinakamalaking budget.

**[00:44:05.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000422)  
Kaya nga, ang pagbawas ng 6 billion sa HGAB,

**[00:44:12.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000423)  
ang bubusisiin sa umagang ito.

**[00:44:14.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000424)  
We must understand what these changes mean on the ground,

**[00:44:19.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000425)  
which schools and learners will be affected,

**[00:44:21.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000426)  
which commitments can still be delivered,

**[00:44:24.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000427)  
and where funding needs to be restored or increased,

**[00:44:27.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000428)  
and what concrete results can DepEd commit to.

**[00:44:30.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000429)  
When we recognize the scale of the task before DepEd,

**[00:44:33.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000430)  
and the dedication of the teachers and persons who keep our schools running,

**[00:44:37.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000431)  
sa huli, ang sukatan ng budget na ito ay makikita sa mismong karanasan,

**[00:44:41.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000432)  
ng ating mga siyudyante.

**[00:44:43.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000433)  
May mga guro bang gumagabay, may kagamitang nagagamit,

**[00:44:46.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000434)  
may ligtas at maayos pang lugar para sa mag-aaral,

**[00:44:49.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000435)  
at may kaalamang bang nagdadagdag sa bawat taon.

**[00:44:53.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000436)  
Every peso invested in education must help make that possible.

**[00:44:57.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000437)  
With that, I yield the floor to our...

**[00:45:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000438)  
subcommittee chair, of course, one of our strongest advocates for education here in the Senate,

**[00:45:07.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000439)  
subcommittee chair, Senator Bam Aquino, to lead today's proceedings.

**[00:45:10.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000440)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:45:11.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000441)  
Thank you, Senator Gachalian.

**[00:45:14.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000442)  
And then we'll have Senator Cayetano.

**[00:45:16.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000443)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:45:17.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000444)  
Mr. Chairman, I just want to show three slides dahil 2026 ay naging napaka-importante

**[00:45:25.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000445)  
at maraming magagandang nangyari for 2026 in the education sector.

**[00:45:31.340] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000446)  
So first of all, nagkaroon po tayo ng isa sa pinakamataas na budget pagdating sa education sector,

**[00:45:37.420] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000447)  
about 1.3 trillion pesos or about 4.36% of GDP.

**[00:45:42.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000448)  
So ito ay pasok na pasok na doon sa requirement ng international standards na 4 to 6% of GDP allocated to education.

**[00:45:52.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000449)  
Pero alam ko marami rin challenges dahil isang...

**[00:45:55.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000450)  
Isa sa pinakamalaking challenges dito ay yung absorptive capacity,

**[00:46:00.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000451)  
kung paano gastusin naman itong pondo na ito.

**[00:46:03.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000452)  
At yan ang pag-uusapan natin ngayong araw na ito.

**[00:46:05.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000453)  
Alam ko binabantayan ho ng ating chairman of the basic education, si Senator Bam Aquino,

**[00:46:13.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000454)  
yung efficient na paggastos nitong budget na ito.

**[00:46:17.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000455)  
Pangalawa, and I have to congratulate the Department of Education under the leadership of Senator Sani Angara,

**[00:46:24.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000456)  
tayo ang most improved country.

**[00:46:25.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000457)  
pagdating sa PISA.

**[00:46:27.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000458)  
At napakalaking bagay ito, both for the education sector and also for national pride.

**[00:46:33.400] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000459)  
Dahil nung unang labas ng PISA, tayo pinakahuli.

**[00:46:37.020] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000460)  
Ngayon, hindi na tayo pinakahuli.

**[00:46:39.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000461)  
Most improved pa.

**[00:46:40.280] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000462)  
So we also want to understand from the Department of Education, yung gains.

**[00:46:45.680] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000463)  
And of course, how to sustain the gains.

**[00:46:48.000] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000464)  
Sana tuloy-tuloy na ho ito pagdating ng mga susunod na panahon.

**[00:46:53.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000465)  
At lastly, on the local...

**[00:46:55.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000466)  
On the local front, yung aral program through the aral law, malaki rin ang improvements pagdating sa literacy.

**[00:47:04.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000467)  
1.3 million students in grades 1 to 10 ang nag-improve.

**[00:47:10.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000468)  
Dati problema natin yung pagbabasa at pagbibilang ngayon ay dahil dito sa aral program,

**[00:47:18.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000469)  
ay malaki ang natulungan natin mga mag-aaral na magbasa sila at marunong magbilang.

**[00:47:25.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000470)  
So we also want to understand how we can sustain this program.

**[00:47:29.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000471)  
Naintindihan ko na hindi nabigyan ng pondo ito this 2027.

**[00:47:35.720] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000472)  
At yun naman ang aming magiging commitment sa Department of Education.

**[00:47:42.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000473)  
Dahil kung maganda yung programa, dapat mapondohan para masustain yung gains nitong programa na ito.

**[00:47:49.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000474)  
So 2026 was a good year for the education sector.

**[00:47:54.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000475)  
Marami tayong makikitang...

**[00:47:55.740] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000476)  
Magagandang nangyayari.

**[00:47:57.760] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000477)  
Ang next challenge natin is paano natin masustain itong mga naumpisahan na natin through the 2027 budget.

**[00:48:05.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000478)  
So with that, I thank the Chairman of the Subcommittee on Education.

**[00:48:10.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-000479)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[00:48:11.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000480)  
Thank you, Senator Gachalian, SP.

**[00:48:13.620] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000481)  
Senator Cayetano.

**[00:48:14.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000482)  
Sir Chairman and Mr. Senate President, with your permission, may I yield because Senator Pia has another appointment.

**[00:48:21.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000483)  
And since she formed part of my education, I will...

**[00:48:25.740] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000484)  
I will pass first the privilege to...

**[00:48:29.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000485)  
Thank you.

**[00:48:30.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000486)  
Senator Pia is recognized.

**[00:48:31.920] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000487)  
Our colleagues know we have some important visitors that I have to attend to.

**[00:48:36.100] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000488)  
I'll make it very brief.

**[00:48:36.960] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000489)  
I just wanted to have the opportunity to greet our Secretary and the whole DepEd team.

**[00:48:41.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000490)  
I miss all of you.

**[00:48:42.320] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000491)  
As you know, I defended the DepEd budget and all the attached agencies when the Secretary was our Chairman of the Committee on Finance.

**[00:48:51.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000492)  
So just very briefly, well, welcome.

**[00:48:55.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000493)  
First of all, and of course, I always tie this into the SDGs, no?

**[00:48:58.980] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000494)  
The Chairman spoke lengthily about stunting and that ties into SDG 2 on zero hunger, SDG 3 on good health and well-being.

**[00:49:09.260] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000495)  
A lot of our health programs are implemented through the schools.

**[00:49:12.560] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000496)  
SDG 5 on gender equality.

**[00:49:14.640] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000497)  
We still have...

**[00:49:15.740] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000498)  
There's so much to say about gender equality, but my biggest issue is how girls are most affected by teen pregnancies.

**[00:49:23.620] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000499)  
Boys are not really...

**[00:49:25.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000500)  
They're not really affected by teen pregnancies.

**[00:49:27.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000501)  
It's the girls who are affected by teen pregnancies.

**[00:49:29.020] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000502)  
So we've got to work harder on that.

**[00:49:31.220] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000503)  
And then SDG 8 on decent work and economic growth.

**[00:49:35.960] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000504)  
Of course, the foundation happens in basic education.

**[00:49:41.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000505)  
And I'll add SDG 11 on safe, resilient, and sustainable cities and communities.

**[00:49:46.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000506)  
As we talk about safeties in the schools, this really affects the entire environment, the entire communities.

**[00:49:53.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000507)  
So I also want to emphasize...

**[00:49:55.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000508)  
On the importance of future-proofing our education, the Secretary and those colleagues of ours who've been around since 2019,

**[00:50:03.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000509)  
I've been harping on this for a long time.

**[00:50:06.160] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000510)  
So now, when I started talking about the future of education, having a futures-thinking mindset, it was kind of vague.

**[00:50:14.600] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000511)  
But now that AI is here, I think it's more...

**[00:50:18.460] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000512)  
It's something easier for us to grasp.

**[00:50:20.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000513)  
And so the challenge is really how to use AI but not let it take over the lives of our children.

**[00:50:25.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000514)  
I'm not here to lecture, but I'm here to listen on how we are engaging with this new development,

**[00:50:33.060] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000515)  
how we can maximize this as a tool for our teachers, for our tool for those who are working in terms of planning,

**[00:50:40.820] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000516)  
the supervisors, the principals, and so on and so forth.

**[00:50:44.200] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000517)  
And I also, again, again, since 2019, want to emphasize in as much as we want,

**[00:50:50.500] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000518)  
we obviously want to see our children reading well and doing math.

**[00:50:55.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000519)  
The four Cs, critical thinking, creativity, collaboration, communications.

**[00:51:02.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000520)  
I started speaking about this around 2019, but now with AI threatening to take over so many jobs,

**[00:51:12.300] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000521)  
it's more important than ever because that's where we now have to make use of this different skill set.

**[00:51:18.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000522)  
And then my last point is early childhood education.

**[00:51:23.340] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000523)  
I'll ask more questions about it later.

**[00:51:25.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000524)  
But I feel like it's an area that we can improve in.

**[00:51:29.440] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000525)  
I'm a little bit worried about it now officially being transferred under DILG.

**[00:51:36.360] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000526)  
I understand the practicality of that.

**[00:51:39.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000527)  
I do get a little bit involved in Taguig and I see how in a city that focuses on education and will spend resources on it,

**[00:51:50.040] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000528)  
then it is a good thing.

**[00:51:51.000] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000529)  
But I really need to understand better how the national government,

**[00:51:55.420] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000530)  
can help those LGUs that may have less resources and worse,

**[00:52:01.240] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000531)  
those that may have some resources but don't choose or do not understand the importance of early childhood education.

**[00:52:11.960] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000532)  
So in Taguig, they have focused on, I say they because I'm not involved in the budget or the planning,

**[00:52:20.780] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000533)  
on...

**[00:52:24.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000535)  
hiring teachers, and this is not the case in most of the daycare centers.

**[00:52:30.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000536)  
You live with what you can, whatever's there.

**[00:52:34.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000537)  
And they're not necessarily teachers.

**[00:52:36.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000538)  
And this is the formative year.

**[00:52:38.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000539)  
So Secretary, I think we need to find out what your thoughts and ideas are on this.

**[00:52:43.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000540)  
And I'll leave it at that.

**[00:52:45.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000541)  
I'll come back after this important meeting and listen.

**[00:52:50.280] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000542)  
Thank you so much, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:52:52.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000543)  
Salamat, Senator Pia.

**[00:52:53.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000544)  
Senator Erwin Tulfo.

**[00:52:54.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000545)  
Thank you.

**[00:52:56.380] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000546)  
Thank you rin, Senator Erwin at Senator Kiko.

**[00:53:00.020] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000547)  
It's okay.

**[00:53:00.660] Senator Pia S. Cayetano** (asr-000548)  
Kasi siya lang naman yung sinisingitan ko, eh.

**[00:53:03.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000549)  
It's alright.

**[00:53:04.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000550)  
It's alright.

**[00:53:05.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000551)  
No problem.

**[00:53:17.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000552)  
Good morning, Mr. Chairman, fellow senators and our officials from the Department of Education.

**[00:53:24.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000553)  
As we deliberate on the proposed 2027 budget of DepEd,

**[00:53:29.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000554)  
I would like to emphasize three priorities that deserve our urgent attention.

**[00:53:34.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000555)  
More classrooms, computers, and safer schools.

**[00:53:38.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000556)  
Una, kailangan nating patuloy na tugunan ang kakulangan sa ating mga silid-aralan.

**[00:53:44.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000557)  
Hindi po dapat patuloy na nagsisiksikan ang ating mga estudyante sa mga silid-aralan,

**[00:53:48.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000558)  
o lumakad ng kilo-kilometro para makapasok lamang sa paaralan,

**[00:53:58.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000559)  
o napipilitang mag-aral sa mga pasilidad na hindi naman angkop sa kanilang pangangailangan.

**[00:54:05.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000560)  
We must prioritize classroom construction, especially in areas where shortages are most severe,

**[00:54:13.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000561)  
so that every Filipino child has access to a decent learning environment.

**[00:54:19.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000562)  
Second, and equally important,

**[00:54:21.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000563)  
is the safety of our children inside school premises.

**[00:54:25.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000564)  
The recent school shooting incidents in school grounds are a painful reminder

**[00:54:31.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000565)  
that schools must be places of learning, not places of fear.

**[00:54:36.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000566)  
Our children should never have to worry about their safety while pursuing their education.

**[00:54:42.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000567)  
Na makatitiyak po ang isang magulang na makakauwi lagi ng ligtas ang kanyang anak mula sa paaralan.

**[00:54:50.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000568)  
Ikatlo at panghuli, may mga mag-aaral po tayo, lalo na sa mga liblib na lugar,

**[00:54:56.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000569)  
na hindi pa po nakakakita ng computer o hindi marunong kung pa paano gamitin ito.

**[00:55:03.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000570)  
As our lives intertwine with cyberspace,

**[00:55:08.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000571)  
our children, particularly in the barrios,

**[00:55:12.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000572)  
should be introduced to the information superhighway and to the artificial intelligence or AI.

**[00:55:19.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000573)  
Kaya nais po natin malaman kung ano mga programa at pondo ang inilaan ng DepEd

**[00:55:25.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000574)  
at ating bigyan ng importansya para palakasin ang school security,

**[00:55:30.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000575)  
improve emergency response, provide psychosocial support,

**[00:55:34.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000576)  
and prevent violence within our schools and computer equipment in every classroom.

**[00:55:41.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000577)  
As we review this budget, let us remember that education is not simply about building classrooms and hiring teachers.

**[00:55:47.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000578)  
It is about

**[00:55:49.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000579)  
building a future for our children and ensuring that they are safe while doing so.

**[00:55:55.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000580)  
Because every peso we allocate must translate into better classrooms,

**[00:56:01.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000581)  
better learning conditions, and safer schools for every Filipino child.

**[00:56:06.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000582)  
Thank you and I look forward to a productive budget hearing. Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[00:56:10.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000583)  
Thank you, Senator Tulfo.

**[00:56:13.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000584)  
Two of the three priorities ni Senator Tulfo, same as my priorities.

**[00:56:17.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000585)  
So for today, we talked about the classrooms, school safety,

**[00:56:22.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000586)  
the computerization budget which was slashed by the House.

**[00:56:26.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000587)  
We talked about teen pregnancies, which is also, I know it's one of the top problems for our youth.

**[00:56:37.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000588)  
I'm sure Senator Kiko will join me in talking about the feeding program.

**[00:56:42.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000589)  
So marami pong focus areas.

**[00:56:44.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000590)  
But my understanding is that the Secretary has,

**[00:56:47.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000591)  
a few slides to start us out.

**[00:56:49.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-000592)  
So, oh, yes, Senator Allan.

**[00:56:52.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000593)  
With your permission, Mr. Chairman.

**[00:56:55.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000594)  
May I follow the President of the Senate President?

**[00:56:59.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000595)  
Just three slides.

**[00:57:01.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000596)  
And follow his President in first and foremost thanking God for the DepEd family,

**[00:57:06.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000597)  
headed by our favorite, of course, Senator Sonny Angara.

**[00:57:12.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000598)  
With him, it's always sunny, never rainy.

**[00:57:17.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000599)  
Nabanggit na ng Senate President

**[00:57:19.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000600)  
yung PISA results and sana many, many more and much more good news to come. Four percent

**[00:57:28.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000601)  
of GDP sa 2026

**[00:57:30.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000602)  
budget, of course, whole of government iyan,

**[00:57:33.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000603)  
including our Senate President who was Chairman of the Finance Committee noon.

**[00:57:37.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000604)  
And 30 to 40% increase in the students' reading proficiency

**[01:01:56.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000697)  
So, the point I want to make, Mr. Senator,

**[01:01:58.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000698)  
Secretary, and I believe your father was one of those who,

**[01:02:03.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000699)

**[01:02:05.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000700)  
pounded it into our heads, di ba, na

**[01:02:08.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000701)

**[01:02:14.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000703)

**[01:02:16.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000704)  
But what our parents pounded into us is that

**[01:02:19.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000705)

**[01:02:22.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000706)  
And during President Pinoy's time and Senator Angara,

**[01:02:26.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000707)  
Ed Angara,

**[01:02:28.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000708)

**[01:02:32.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000709)

**[01:02:33.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000710)

**[01:02:37.380] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000711)

but maybe another 500 billion.

**[01:02:50.980] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000715)

**[01:02:52.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000716)

**[01:02:57.800] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000718)

**[01:02:58.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000719)  
Focusing where it's needed to be focused.

Having said that, may I add to the recommendation

LGU-based.

LGU-based.

DPWH.

And PPP.

In my consultations with LGUs,

new schools are easier than school-based.

The buildings.

**[01:03:29.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000732)  
Because sa school buildings ngayon,

**[01:03:31.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000733)

may I ask if...

may I propose later on na

**[01:03:55.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000741)  
whether it's 100 million, 500 million,

**[01:03:57.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000742)  
that you offer LGUs where it is very crowded and there are two or three shifting, no?

**[01:04:03.980] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000743)  
So meaning, we'll say, for 200 million, you have to have at least ganito karaming classroom for new school.

**[01:04:10.500] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000744)  
Sila na bahala lahat. They'll look for the land, they'll clear it, they'll put it up, etc.

**[01:04:15.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000745)  
That might be just really much faster.

**[01:04:19.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000746)  
And if you look at the population growth in Central Luzon, Metro Manila, and Calabar Zone,

**[01:04:25.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000747)  
I mean, everyone's here, new schools are definitely needed.

**[01:04:29.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000748)  
My last point is really to address the threat, and I do know everyone's doing this.

**[01:04:35.040] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000749)  
So like sa stunting, but going back, if yung feeding project, kalahati yung tinanggal,

how do we address that stunting?

**[01:04:44.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000751)  
Disabilities, you know, unintended consequences.

**[01:04:49.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000752)  
When Mayor Lani became Mayor ng Taguig, her intention was to cut the lines in the health center.

**[01:04:55.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000753)  
Kasi dinoblay yung budget ng gamot, ganun pa rin yung lines.

**[01:04:59.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000754)  
Dinoblay na naman yung budget, ganun pa rin yung lines.

**[01:05:02.500] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000755)  
So when she started delivering the maintenance medicine, high blood,

**[01:05:10.060] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000756)  
and iba kahit sa chronic like sa hika, in house to house, diniliver, nabawasan yung lines.

**[01:05:15.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000757)  
So naiwan sa lines mga bata.

**[01:05:17.740] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000758)  
But when they started fixing up the clinics and doing the eye and ear check up in the schools,

**[01:05:24.020] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000759)  
hindi lang nabawasan.

**[01:05:25.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000760)  
Hindi lang nabawasan yung lines sa health center.

**[01:05:28.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000761)  
Many, many teachers and parents discovered that their children weren't slow learners.

**[01:05:35.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000762)

**[01:05:37.300] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000763)  
It's that they had poor eyesight or problems with hearing.

**[01:05:42.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000764)  
So I do know it's a good suggestion, but where do you get the money?

**[01:05:46.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000765)  
But just like APECO, which was a, I'll end with that.

**[01:05:50.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000766)  
There are sometimes one thing that changes everything.

**[01:05:54.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000767)  
Di ba?

**[01:05:55.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000768)  
Aurora is beautiful, but when APECO came, it changed the economics.

**[01:05:59.980] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000769)  
Di ba?

**[01:06:00.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000770)  
Tagig, binabaha, et cetera, it was really prayer, dedication to God.

**[01:06:04.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000771)  
When BGC came, nag-iba.

**[01:06:09.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000772)  
Di ba?

**[01:06:10.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000773)  
When call centers came to the Philippines, suddenly hindi lang OFWs.

**[01:06:14.740] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000774)  
So there has to be this one thing that can be done in 2027.

**[01:06:22.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000775)  
And I'll remind Senator Angara something.

**[01:06:24.880] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000776)  
Something that had an impact on me, Secretary Angara.

**[01:06:28.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000777)  
When he was a new senator, binitbit niya ako sa dinner ata yun with the British Embassy.

**[01:06:35.260] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000778)  
Because nga of the, yung mag-amang Senator Angara,

**[01:06:38.300] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000779)  
have a lot of background and projects and work with the United Kingdom.

**[01:06:43.680] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000780)  
And there was a military expert there, if you remember.

**[01:06:47.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000781)  
And he was telling us, di ba?

**[01:06:49.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000782)  
When they were saying that may decline na yung empire of the United Kingdom,

**[01:06:54.880] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000783)  
and they had to scale down the military,

**[01:06:58.740] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000784)  
the first thinking was really, how do you not do it?

**[01:07:02.700] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000785)  
Di ba?

**[01:07:02.860] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000786)  
How do you maintain your bases in Singapore and the other colonies?

**[01:07:06.980] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000787)  
How do you have more money?

**[01:07:08.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000788)  
How do you have your economy?

**[01:07:09.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000789)  
But as time and technology changed,

**[01:07:13.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000790)  
they said, no, it's not about bigger, it's also about smarter.

**[01:07:17.800] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000791)  
Di ba?

**[01:07:18.040] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000792)  
Mas mission-oriented.

**[01:07:19.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000793)  
And he told us a couple of things like,

**[01:07:21.460] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000794)  
like halimbawa, the next war.

**[01:07:24.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000795)  
This was,

**[01:07:24.880] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000796)  
2011 siguro, or 2010 ka na-elect ka, di ba?

**[01:07:28.460] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000797)  
Yeah, 2013.

**[01:07:29.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000798)  
So 2013, 2014 ito.

**[01:07:32.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000799)  
And they were telling us na the next war,

**[01:07:36.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000800)  
and this was 12 years ago, no?

**[01:07:38.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000801)  
We will be a lot cyber.

**[01:07:41.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000802)  
So they converted many of their units to, you know,

**[01:07:46.020] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000803)  
having so much equipment, so much people, so much bases.

**[01:07:49.260] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000804)  
So sabi nila na, if you look at our military now,

**[01:07:53.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000805)

**[01:07:54.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000806)

**[01:07:56.800] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000807)

**[01:07:59.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000808)  
So let me leave that challenge to all of us, to all of us, no?

**[01:08:04.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000809)

**[01:08:07.040] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000810)

**[01:08:10.300] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000811)

**[01:08:22.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000816)

**[01:08:24.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-000817)  
Thank you, Mr. Chairman. Thank you, Secretary.

**[01:10:34.620] Presentation video** (asr-000872)  
Sa nakalipas na dalawang taon,

**[01:10:36.620] Presentation video** (asr-000873)  
may mga pagbabagong nagsimula

**[01:10:38.620] Presentation video** (asr-000874)  
ng maramdaman sa mga paaralan.

**[01:10:43.620] Presentation video** (asr-000875)  
Sa early age niya,

**[01:10:44.620] Presentation video** (asr-000876)  
kahit napakahaba nung name niya,

**[01:10:46.620] Presentation video** (asr-000877)  
alam niya na po paano magsulat.

**[01:10:48.620] Presentation video** (asr-000878)  
Ilala niya na rin po lahat ng letters

**[01:10:50.620] Presentation video** (asr-000879)  
at lahat po ng sound.

**[01:10:52.620] Presentation video** (asr-000880)  
Wala po sa pinakamalapit na public school

**[01:10:55.620] Presentation video** (asr-000881)  
yung track na gusto ko.

**[01:10:56.620] Presentation video** (asr-000882)  
And yung voucher naman po,

**[01:10:57.620] Presentation video** (asr-000883)  
nakakalesan po siya ng financial burden

**[01:11:00.620] Presentation video** (asr-000884)  
ng family ko.

**[01:11:02.620] Presentation video** (asr-000885)  
Ang kumot niya may anim na laso.

**[01:11:08.620] Presentation video** (asr-000886)  
Noon po hindi po po marunong magbasa,

**[01:11:12.620] Presentation video** (asr-000887)  
nahihiya po ako.

**[01:11:13.620] Presentation video** (asr-000888)  
Tapos yung marunong magbasa,

**[01:11:15.620] Presentation video** (asr-000889)  
hindi na po maghihiya.

**[01:11:17.620] Presentation video** (asr-000890)  
Tapos masaya na po ako.

**[01:11:20.620] Presentation video** (asr-000891)  
Dahil po sa bago classroom ako,

**[01:11:22.620] Presentation video** (asr-000892)  
mas enjoy po ako mag-aral

**[01:11:24.620] Presentation video** (asr-000893)  
ng mabuti para magiging doktor paglaki.

**[01:11:32.620] Presentation video** (asr-000894)  
Kung ngayon na may internet na ang school,

**[01:11:34.620] Presentation video** (asr-000895)  
mas natututunan nila yung lessons nila na mabilis.

**[01:11:36.620] Presentation video** (asr-000896)  
At sa mga visual learners namin,

**[01:11:38.620] Presentation video** (asr-000897)  
nakikita nila using internet

**[01:11:40.620] Presentation video** (asr-000898)  
kung paano yung mga lessons na-apply sa totoong buhay.

**[01:11:42.620] Presentation video** (asr-000899)  
Isa lang naman ang kagustuhan po ng bawat guru.

**[01:11:45.620] Presentation video** (asr-000900)  
Yan ay maiangat ang mga kabataan.

**[01:11:48.620] Presentation video** (asr-000901)  
Naniniwala po kami na ang mga batang Pilipino

**[01:11:50.620] Presentation video** (asr-000902)  
ay talagang tunay na

**[01:11:52.620] Presentation video** (asr-000903)  
matatalino.

**[01:12:02.620] Presentation video** (asr-000904)  
Napaka-fulfilling po sa feeling na

**[01:12:05.620] Presentation video** (asr-000905)  
grabe, nakaka-contribute po ako sa international level na assessment

**[01:12:09.620] Presentation video** (asr-000906)  
to prove na meron talaga quality education sa Pilipinas

**[01:12:12.620] Presentation video** (asr-000907)  
and also merong improvement when it comes to teaching, learning,

**[01:12:17.620] Presentation video** (asr-000908)  
and also applying those learning.

**[01:12:20.620] Presentation video** (asr-000909)  
Kapag mas matibay ang simula,

**[01:12:22.620] Presentation video** (asr-000910)  
mas maraming pagkakataon ang nagbubukas.

**[01:12:25.620] Presentation video** (asr-000911)  
Mas maganda kasi po yung new curriculum.

**[01:12:28.620] Presentation video** (asr-000912)  
Lalo natin naman po pag itating namin sa graduate school,

**[01:12:30.620] Presentation video** (asr-000913)  
may tinatawag na doorway option.

**[01:12:32.620] Presentation video** (asr-000914)  
Na pwede kaming kumuha ng isang elective subject kung gusto namin po.

**[01:12:36.620] Presentation video** (asr-000915)  
Maganda siya para sa akin kasi nakakatulong po ako sa pamilya ko

**[01:12:39.620] Presentation video** (asr-000916)  
and I'm currently now a makeup artist po.

**[01:12:41.620] Presentation video** (asr-000917)  
Pero walang batang umaabot doon ng mag-isa.

**[01:12:45.620] Presentation video** (asr-000918)  
Kailangan niya ng gurong may sapat na oras at suporta.

**[01:12:50.620] Presentation video** (asr-000919)  
Kasi full load, daming gawain.

**[01:12:53.620] Presentation video** (asr-000920)  
Misal na pero kailangan ko pa rin tapusin.

**[01:12:56.620] Presentation video** (asr-000921)  
Pero ngayon naging mas magaan,

**[01:12:58.620] Presentation video** (asr-000922)  
mas nakapokus na ako sa aking tungkulin bilang guro sa mga bata.

**[01:13:02.620] Presentation video** (asr-000923)  
Sa wakas po, mapapagaan po nito ang aming trabaho

**[01:13:06.620] Presentation video** (asr-000924)  
at makakapag-focus po kami sa pagtuturo po namin.

**[01:13:12.620] Presentation video** (asr-000925)  
Siyempre, nandyan yung number one band paper.

**[01:13:14.620] Presentation video** (asr-000926)  
Ink for printer namin.

**[01:13:16.620] Presentation video** (asr-000927)  
Kahit pa isa-isang bilihan o.

**[01:13:18.620] Presentation video** (asr-000928)  
Pag binilang mo at pinagsama-sama, marami din siya at malaki talaga.

**[01:13:22.620] Presentation video** (asr-000929)  
We are grateful na nanagdagan yung teaching allowance namin

**[01:13:25.620] Presentation video** (asr-000930)  
kasi malaking tulong para sa gayon

**[01:13:28.620] Presentation video** (asr-000931)  
mas maturuan at maibigay namin yung pangangailangan ng mga bata.

**[01:13:32.620] Presentation video** (asr-000932)  
At hindi lang trabaho ang kailangang gumaan.

**[01:13:36.620] Presentation video** (asr-000933)  
Kailangan ding magaroon ng pagkakataong umunlad ang guro.

**[01:13:40.620] Presentation video** (asr-000934)  
Ngayon ay nakikita na rin namin ang aming landas

**[01:13:44.620] Presentation video** (asr-000935)  
para sa aming growth or pagunlad.

**[01:13:46.620] Presentation video** (asr-000936)  
Along-along na sa aming paghanda.

**[01:13:49.620] Presentation video** (asr-000937)  
There are times na nakakalimutan rin namin yung sarili naming well-being,

**[01:13:53.620] Presentation video** (asr-000938)  
sarili naming health.

**[01:13:54.620] Presentation video** (asr-000939)  
With this medical allowance,

**[01:13:56.620] Presentation video** (asr-000940)  
nagkaroon kami ng pakiramdam na the government is also looking at our physical well-being.

**[01:14:03.620] Presentation video** (asr-000941)  
At sa paralan, ang pag-aalaga ay hindi nagtatapos sa pagtuturo.

**[01:14:09.620] Presentation video** (asr-000942)  
Sa hirap talaga ng buhay ngayon, talagang masasabi ko napakalaking tulong

**[01:14:15.620] Presentation video** (asr-000943)  
para sa ating mga magulang ng feeding program na iyon.

**[01:14:18.620] Presentation video** (asr-000944)  
Minsan, may mga bagay na hindi mo kayang sabihin sa parents and friends mo

**[01:14:23.620] Presentation video** (asr-000945)  
at nakakagaan po ng loob kapag may taong nakikinig at may concerns sa iyo.

**[01:14:29.620] Presentation video** (asr-000946)  
Bilang magulang, kampante kami dahil hindi lang natututukan ang kanilang pag-aaral,

**[01:14:37.620] Presentation video** (asr-000947)  
kundi ang kanilang kaligtasan.

**[01:14:39.620] Presentation video** (asr-000948)  
Kahit may sakuna, kami ng mga guro at mga bata ay alam na po,

**[01:14:43.620] Presentation video** (asr-000949)  
yung mga paghahanda at dapat gawin.

**[01:14:46.620] Presentation video** (asr-000950)  
Sa katunayan, may mga safety measures na po kami.

**[01:14:49.620] Presentation video** (asr-000951)  
At para magawa ito, dapat maihatid ang mga pangangailangan ng bawat paaralan.

**[01:14:56.620] Presentation video** (asr-000952)  
Ang maganda yung epekto, mabilis at saka maayos.

**[01:15:00.000] Presentation video** (asr-000953)  
yung dating ng mga learning materials sa school.

**[01:15:03.400] Presentation video** (asr-000954)  
Kasi nagiging masaya yung mga estudyante, yung mga teachers.

**[01:15:09.740] Presentation video** (asr-000955)  
Ang daming tumutulong sa ating paaralan,

**[01:15:12.300] Presentation video** (asr-000956)  
yung ating mga stakeholders na laging nandyan

**[01:15:14.500] Presentation video** (asr-000957)  
para tumulong sa mga needs ng school.

**[01:15:16.900] Presentation video** (asr-000958)  
Pero mas napapadali ang pagtulong kapag sentralisado

**[01:15:21.160] Presentation video** (asr-000959)  
at malalaman na mismo kung anong pangangailangan ng paaralan.

**[01:15:24.960] Presentation video** (asr-000960)  
Mas panatagpo kami ngayon dahil ramdam namin

**[01:15:27.980] Presentation video** (asr-000961)

**[01:15:31.300] Presentation video** (asr-000962)

**[01:15:35.400] Presentation video** (asr-000963)  
May mga nasimulan na at mas marami pa tayong gagawin.

**[01:15:40.760] Presentation video** (asr-000964)  
Edukasyon pa rin ang siyang pangunahin nagtataguyod ng kakayahan.

**[01:15:45.400] Presentation video** (asr-000965)  
Kung kaya ito ang nangunguna sa listahan ng administrasyong ito.

**[01:15:51.300] Presentation video** (asr-000966)  
Kaya malinaw ang susunod na hapbang,

**[01:15:55.180] Presentation video** (asr-000967)

**[01:15:56.600] Presentation video** (asr-000968)  
at palawakin ang naaabot.

of the...

**[01:17:56.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001006)  
of the DepEd proper and the attached agencies broken down

**[01:18:07.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001008)

**[01:18:10.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001009)

**[01:18:14.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001010)

**[01:18:26.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001014)

**[01:18:26.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001015)  
This slide shows the comparison between the 2026 GAAP and General Appropriations Act

**[01:18:34.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001016)

**[01:18:37.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001017)

**[01:18:45.020] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001018)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:18:47.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001019)  
So, this slide would show the General Appropriations Bill highlights,

**[01:18:52.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001020)  
and they're divided into four quadrants, so to speak.

**[01:18:55.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001021)  
So, ando dun po yung personnel

**[01:18:56.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001022)  
improvement.

**[01:18:57.700] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001025)  
The expanded career progression is also a reform that has been championed

**[01:19:03.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001026)  
by His Honor and the Senate President and this body.

**[01:19:08.380] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001027)  
So, it's getting an unprecedented level of funding at 12.09,

**[01:19:12.920] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001028)  
and we'll be able to promote 135,000 personnel.

**[01:19:17.020] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001029)  
The capacity building continues, and the RL tutors,

**[01:19:20.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001030)  
this is something long awaited by our people in the field.

**[01:19:24.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001031)  
Next is the education and system improvement,

**[01:19:26.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001032)  
So there's 6.4 billion for textbooks and instructional materials, plus these a loan for learning upgrade support, 9.4 billion, and then our special education programs for inclusive education including SNED, IPED, madrasa, multi-grade would total 2.78 billion.

**[01:19:46.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001033)  
And the third area is improving the learning environment. So for 3,800 classrooms, that would total 13.4 billion. Repairing 11,000 classrooms, 7.3 billion. ICT solutions and capacity building, 2.6 billion. That was cut. 3.1 billion was cut from that.

**[01:20:07.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001034)  
One point, additional security guards for 500 billion was just an addition made by the house.

**[01:20:14.020] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001035)  
Then in the area of learner support and well-being,

**[01:20:16.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001036)  
Mr. Chair, nabanggit ninyong nutritious meals for kindergarten down from this year, as you mentioned, to 11.4 billion.

**[01:20:25.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001037)  
And then yung voucher grantees naman, 2.47, 40 billion rather. I think ito din nagdagan ng 1.9 billion ng house in line with the bill you have sponsored on the K-6 expansion ng vouchers po, Your Honor.

**[01:20:45.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001038)  
Tapos yung sa...

**[01:20:46.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001039)  
bagong hire na school counselor associates and SDO counselors pursuant to the law, the mental health in basic education, which the Senate President sponsored and his honor also championed, 801 million.

**[01:21:00.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001040)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:21:03.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001041)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:21:05.020] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001042)  
Itong school-based feeding program natin, diyan pinapakita yung impact evaluation ng ating school feeding program, which you really helped dramatically raise.

**[01:21:16.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001043)  
Your Honor, this body helped raise the funding for.

**[01:21:21.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001044)  
So, itong kita, before feeding for school year 2024-25, makikita ho sa kaliwa.

**[01:21:28.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001045)  
Pagka nung tinimbang ho yung ating mga bata, 1.73 million ang severely wasted and wasted.

**[01:21:37.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001046)  
Then yung sa kanan niya, after feeding, naging 680,000 na lang.

**[01:21:41.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001047)  
So, mahigit sa isang milyong bata ang natanggal dun sa...

**[01:21:46.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001048)  
wasted or severely wasted, Your Honor.

**[01:21:49.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001049)  
Bukod doon, syempre yung well-documented din yung benefits

**[01:21:52.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001050)  
in terms of learning gains and in terms of attendance, Your Honor.

**[01:21:57.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001051)  
So then in the same, the one on the right also shows the benefits

**[01:22:02.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001052)  
from the feeding before and after for the next school year, Your Honor.

**[01:22:08.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001053)  
So yung effect po ng reform ninyo is increasing the beneficiaries

**[01:22:16.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001054)  
of the feeding program by 1.2 million, 3.4 to 4.64, Your Honor.

**[01:22:25.080] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001055)  
And of course, we'd like to acknowledge also the reforms

**[01:22:27.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001056)  
under the Sagip-Saka law authored by Senator Kiko Pangilinan.

**[01:22:31.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001057)  
We're trying to move towards increased local procurement.

**[01:22:34.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001058)  
And Senator Kiko has been helping us with pointing out

**[01:22:39.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001059)  
who are the cooperatives in various areas that they work with

**[01:22:42.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001060)  
in his capacity as chair of the Committee on Agriculture.

**[01:22:45.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001061)  
So nagpapasalamat.

**[01:22:46.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001062)  
Kamatu kami doon sa tulong.

**[01:22:48.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001063)  
And we're really trying to procure locally also,

**[01:22:51.080] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001064)  
not only to reduce the carbon footprint,

**[01:22:54.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001065)  
but also to keep our farmers and cooperatives alive and thriving, Your Honor.

**[01:23:01.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001066)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:23:03.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001067)  
So ito pong, we were asked by the committee, Mr. Chair, Mr. President,

**[01:23:07.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001068)  
to prepare some scenarios for ito pong yung various scenarios

**[01:23:11.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001069)  
because we were given a GAB-level budget of 11.4 billion.

**[01:23:15.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001070)  
This covers, as the chair mentioned, 25 pesos per meal

**[01:23:19.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001071)  
for 3.7 million learners for 120 days.

**[01:23:23.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001072)  
So this includes universal kindergarten and then pursuant to our feeding law,

**[01:23:27.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001073)  
severely wasted and wasted for grades 1 to 6.

**[01:23:32.100] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001074)  
And adolescent mothers pala.

**[01:23:33.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001075)  
This is also a reform pushed by EDCOM to cover those who are expectant mothers.

**[01:23:40.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001076)  
So itong, there are four options here, Your Honor.

**[01:23:44.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001077)  
Your Honor.

**[01:23:45.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001078)  
If you choose 180 days at 25 pesos per meal,

**[01:23:48.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001079)  
if you go for 120 to 180,

**[01:23:50.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001080)  
that will require the following amounts.

**[01:23:54.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001081)  
For universal kinder, 3.7, additional 9.5, and so on and so forth.

**[01:24:00.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001082)  
Of course, the most ambitious here would be to move to 30 pesos at 200 days,

**[01:24:09.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001083)  
which is the full school year.

**[01:24:11.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001084)  
That would require an additional 46.6 billion.

**[01:24:14.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001085)  
So that's there.

**[01:24:15.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001086)  
It's quite a costly investment.

**[01:24:20.620] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001087)  
But I saw former Economic Planning Secretary Habito talking about it on a show the other day

**[01:24:27.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001088)  
that this is one of the biggest investments we can make as a country.

**[01:24:31.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001089)  
Although maaring hindi natin makita kaagad yung resulta,

**[01:24:34.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001090)  
definitely yun ay nagawa ng mga progressive mga bansa.

**[01:24:39.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001091)  
So again, thank you to this body for the support on that

**[01:24:43.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001092)  
and for any support that may be forthcoming.

**[01:24:45.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001093)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:24:47.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001094)  
The Chair also mentioned school safety and security.

**[01:24:51.060] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001095)  
So nag-issue ho tayo ng panibagong DepEd Order No. 006 Guidelines on Ensuring a Safe and Motivating Learning Environment.

**[01:25:01.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001096)  
So bukod dun sa tighter security measures sa ating mga eskwelahan,

**[01:25:05.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001097)  
more CCTV cameras, more metal detectors, improved fencing, more security guards,

**[01:25:12.380] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001098)  
nandun din po yung mental health and safety.

**[01:25:15.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001099)  
Support, psychological support sa ating mga bata.

**[01:25:19.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001100)  
Learner support in the form of sports, scouting, school clubs.

**[01:25:26.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001101)  
Meron tayong national and regional telesafe hotline.

**[01:25:29.080] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001102)  
Nag-hire na po tayo ng 5,400 school counselor associates.

**[01:25:32.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001103)  
Again, pursuant to the law passed by this body, the Mental Health in Basic Education Law.

**[01:25:39.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001104)  
So kami nagpapasalamat dun dahil well-documented din ang EDCOG 2 yung kakulangan.

**[01:25:45.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001105)  
Ito po yung sistema natin ng mga guidance counselors.

**[01:25:48.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001106)  
And this has proven to be a good workaround, institutional.

**[01:25:52.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001107)  
Otherwise, dead-end hood talaga tayo dito.

**[01:25:55.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001108)  
But now, we gave an oath to hundreds of school counselor associates yesterday.

**[01:26:01.080] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001109)  
And they're very young. They're in their 20s, fresh graduates.

**[01:26:03.920] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001110)  
And they look very excited to help our learners.

**[01:26:06.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001111)  
Thank you to this body for that.

**[01:26:08.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001112)  
Next slide, please.

**[01:26:10.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001113)  
Ito po yung updates on incidents of school violence.

**[01:26:13.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001114)  
Of course, nabanggit din yung tatlong insidente.

**[01:26:15.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001115)  
Sa Tacloban, sa Sambuanga, sa South Cotabato.

**[01:26:19.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001116)  
Well, our injured learners have all been discharged.

**[01:26:23.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001117)  
And really, lost in the news sometimes are the heroic efforts of some of our students

**[01:26:30.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001118)  
that prevented further loss of life, Your Honor.

**[01:26:33.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001119)  
So binigyan natin ang parangal.

**[01:26:35.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001120)  
Binigyan din ang Boy Scouts of the Philippines ng parangal yung two learners natin

**[01:26:39.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001121)  
sa Tacloban at saka sa South Cotabato.

**[01:26:43.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001122)  
So, again, this incident also highlighted the need for greater psychological first aid,

**[01:26:51.060] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001123)  
mental health awareness.

**[01:26:53.380] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001124)  
So, going forward, ang gusto po natin is mas marami tayong matrain

**[01:26:58.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001125)  
ng mga teachers natin or educators sa larangan po ng mental health and psychological first aid.

**[01:27:05.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001126)  
And we're thankful to the DSWD, DOH for helping us, assisting us, and many in the private sector also.

**[01:27:13.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001127)  
So, for assisting us here.

**[01:27:15.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001128)  
Next slide, please.

Nabanggit ni Chair yung infrastructure budget.

Ang total infrastructure budget is 89.5 billion.

**[01:27:26.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001132)  
New construction, 65.9 billion.

**[01:27:30.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001133)  
Repair, 7.7 billion.

**[01:27:32.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001134)

**[01:27:36.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001135)  
Nakalista doon school furniture, quick response, completion, mid-rise building, gabal doon.

**[01:27:42.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001136)  
Library hub.

**[01:27:51.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001138)  
So, yun po ang breakdown nun.

**[01:28:15.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001144)

**[01:28:43.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001149)

**[01:28:50.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001151)  
And ang na-obligate po natin dito is around 60%.

**[01:28:53.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001152)  
And with a disbursement of 37.

**[01:28:57.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001153)  
The DPWH-led implementation, as mentioned, covers 6,000 classrooms.

**[01:29:02.760] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001154)

Sa LGU-s po.

**[01:29:12.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001156)

**[01:29:13.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001157)

**[01:29:20.920] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001158)

**[01:35:03.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001269)

**[01:35:06.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001270)

**[01:35:11.340] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001271)

**[01:35:15.340] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001272)

**[01:35:22.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001275)

**[01:35:23.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001276)

**[01:35:26.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001277)

**[01:35:27.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001278)

**[01:35:28.700] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001279)

**[01:35:31.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001280)

**[01:35:33.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001281)  
But ito po nga, 147.9 billion, including our attached agencies.

**[01:35:38.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001282)  
So, for learning recovery, the ARAL has not received any allocation.

**[01:35:41.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001283)  
So, we're asking for 15.6 billion, 1.9 billion.

**[01:35:46.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001284)  
1.09 billion is the honoraria for teaching overload.

**[01:35:49.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001285)  
Kasi hindi po makaka...

**[01:35:50.760] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001286)  
If the ARAL tutor is a teacher,

**[01:35:52.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001287)  
they will not be entitled to the additional compensation

**[01:35:56.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001288)  
unless it's an overload pay,

**[01:35:58.700] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001289)  
they have to go do it over on top of the 6R required by the Magna Carta Law for teachers, Your Honor.

**[01:36:05.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001290)  
So, there is training for DepEd tutors of 1.75 billion.

**[01:36:08.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001291)

**[01:36:15.100] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001292)  
ARAL resources, kasi nagpiprint po tayo ng resources, 4.6 billion.

**[01:36:19.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001293)  
Summer program, 1.2 billion.

**[01:36:23.060] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001294)  
Learning recovery and remediation consultation, 6 billion.

**[01:36:25.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001295)  
Ito po yung mas malaking bagay pag pinakain po.

**[01:36:28.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001296)  
Mayroon natin at binigyan natin ng food allowance, yung mga teachers natin.

**[01:36:32.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001297)
