**[01:36:37.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001298)  
And then we have the Bayang Bumabasa Initiative, led by EDCOM, 50 million.

**[01:36:41.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001299)  
And for personal improvement, we have 52.1 billion with the teachers, the PDOs.

**[01:36:46.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001300)  
And all of this is the vision of our president na talagang yung mga teachers makapag-concentrate sa pagtuturo.

**[01:36:52.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001301)  
Kaya lahat ng school sana, mayroon tayong project development officer, mayroon tayong admin officer to sit.

**[01:36:58.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001302)  
yung mga non-teaching-related tasks, sila na po ang gagawa.

**[01:37:03.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001303)  
Then we have benefits and personnel support, yung overtime pay,

**[01:37:07.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001304)  
terminal leave benefits, laptops for teachers and staff, teacher training.

**[01:37:13.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001305)

**[01:37:17.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001306)  
classroom repairs, last-mile school buildings, pursuant to the law you passed,

**[01:37:22.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001307)  
50 Gabaldon restorations, pursuant to the Gabaldon law,

**[01:37:26.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001308)  
disaster preparedness, that I think we're really looking at that,

**[01:37:32.760] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001309)  
seeing how many disasters have hit us in the past few years.

**[01:37:38.760] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001310)  
Textbooks and instructional materials, ito po yung nirecommend ho ng EDCOM na

**[01:37:43.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001311)  
bawat bata merong workbook, merong textbook, bukod sa textbook, may workbook siya na pwede niyang sulatan

**[01:37:52.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001312)  
at pwede niyang iuwi at hindi na po ipapasa-pasa sana.

**[01:37:56.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001313)  
Ibang batch.

**[01:38:00.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001314)  
And of course, we're asking for additional for security guards, for metal detectors,

**[01:38:05.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001315)  
for perimeter fences, Your Honor, which were not really included in the budget previously.

**[01:38:10.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001316)

**[01:38:12.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001317)  
Ito po yung second page, learner support and well-being.

**[01:38:15.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001318)

**[01:38:21.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001319)  
to have greater coverage ng Key Stage 1.

**[01:38:26.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001320)

**[01:38:32.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001321)

**[01:38:43.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001322)  
So various programs, learner support programs for sports, for the Palarong Pambansa,

**[01:38:48.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001323)  
for mental health and guidance counseling, totaling 13 billion.

**[01:38:53.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001324)

**[01:38:56.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001327)

**[01:39:03.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001328)

**[01:39:06.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001329)

**[01:39:16.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001330)  
especially in terms of ASEAN and syncing with the world qualifications.

**[01:39:21.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001331)  
We're playing catch-up here.

**[01:39:22.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001332)

**[01:39:26.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001333)  
Senator Allen.

**[01:39:27.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001334)

**[01:39:34.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001335)

**[01:39:37.620] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001336)  
And then next slide, please.

**[01:39:39.060] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001337)  
For our attached agencies also, meron din silang mga request.

**[01:39:42.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001338)

**[01:39:52.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001339)  
And of course, we recall the Philippines was the featured country.

**[01:39:56.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001340)

**[01:40:00.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001341)

**[01:40:05.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001342)

**[01:40:12.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001343)

**[01:40:21.100] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001344)

**[01:40:26.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001345)

**[01:40:36.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001346)

**[01:40:44.340] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001347)

**[01:40:56.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001349)

**[01:41:02.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001350)

**[01:41:05.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001351)

**[01:41:43.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001355)

**[01:41:56.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001356)

**[01:42:00.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001357)

**[01:42:02.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001358)

**[01:42:10.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001359)

**[01:42:15.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001360)

**[01:42:23.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001361)

**[01:42:26.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001362)  
education, and graduate scholarship, and regional hubs, which are mandates of the law.

**[01:42:30.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001363)

**[01:42:34.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001364)

This is a LEDOC, as Secretary Sani knows and the Senate President knows, this is a LEDOC measure.

**[02:00:07.220] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001729)  
And the feeding program is probably one of our biggest contributions to decrease our learning gap.

**[02:00:17.960] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001730)  
Kung mapondohan po natin ito at ma-implement natin na maayos,

**[02:00:23.520] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001731)  
many of our issues in our schools should be solved.

**[02:00:27.380] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001732)  
Or this will help in a big way yung mga problema po ng mga bata pag-ating sa pagtuto.

**[02:00:34.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001733)  
So may I ask, Secretary, or baka si Yusek Cabral might want to be the one to answer,

**[02:00:42.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001734)  
the huge jump in the budget, sir, kayo yun diba? Kayo yun sa feeding program, right?

**[02:00:47.480] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001735)  
Ah, si Asik Roger po.

**[02:00:48.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001736)  
Ah, si Asik Roger pala yun.

**[02:00:51.260] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001737)  
Sorry, medyo magkalupalay kasi na konti.

**[02:00:53.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001738)  
Sorry, Yusek Cabral.

**[02:00:55.760] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001739)  
Asik Roger, you may ano?

**[02:00:57.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001740)  
Pwede kayong sumingit dito kung gusto nyo kay Yusek Ron, if you wish to answer.

**[02:01:01.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001741)  
May I know, what were the biggest challenges?

**[02:01:04.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001742)  
Kasi ang laki po ng jump ng pondo in 2026 sa feeding program.

**[02:01:10.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001743)  
Were you able to properly absorb it, distribute the nutritious food?

**[02:01:18.660] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001744)  
Para lang guided po kami.

**[02:01:20.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001745)  
Kasi ako, personally, sasabihin ko right now, intention ko talaga to really push for the increase.

**[02:01:26.000] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001746)  
Especially that we have the...

**[02:01:27.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001747)  
the Expanded National Nutrition Program or yung Librim Pagkain para sa Batang Pilipino Act in the works.

**[02:01:33.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001748)  
In fact, it's almost done.

**[02:01:35.620] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001749)  
Nasa BICAM stage na po ito.

**[02:01:37.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001750)  
So, could you help us, Asik Roger?

**[02:01:40.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001751)  
Yung naging malaking talon sa pondo ngayong taon,

**[02:01:44.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001752)  
were you able to successfully implement the feeding program as stated in the GAA?

**[02:01:50.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001753)  
Kasi sa GAA po, Universal K, Universal Grade 1 at 23 pesos.

**[02:01:57.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001754)  
25 pesos.

**[02:01:59.660] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001755)  
Were we able to implement that this year?

**[02:02:01.780] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001756)  
I see that we have gains eh, diba?

**[02:02:03.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001757)  
So, I'm sure there's a level of implementation we were able to do.

**[02:02:06.580] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001758)  
But, could you help us, Asik Roger?

**[02:02:08.940] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001759)  
Yes po. Good morning po, Mr. Chair.

**[02:02:13.300] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001760)  
Allocated na po siya.

**[02:02:14.540] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001761)  
Tapos, ang kaibahan po ngayon is that nasa division office na po yung pera.

**[02:02:19.800] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001762)  
So, kung mayroon tayong 220 na kabayo, lahat na po ito tumatakbo ngayon.

**[02:02:23.900] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001763)  
So, lahat na po ng division ay nag-iimplementa po.

**[02:02:27.200] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001764)  
Ng feeding program.

**[02:02:28.940] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001765)  
Pero, different po yung phasing nila.

**[02:02:30.560] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001766)  
Kasi may naon ang nag-procure.

**[02:02:32.480] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001767)  
Mayroong nahuli ng konti.

**[02:02:34.040] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001768)  
Pero, lahat na po sila tumatakbo.

**[02:02:36.200] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001769)  
Yung difference po ngayon, Mr. Chair,

**[02:02:38.320] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001770)  
kasi mayroon, may ringgan mo kami ng 1 billion for the central kitchen.

**[02:02:43.680] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001771)  
So, ito po ay for ano na po?

**[02:02:45.380] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001772)  
For downloading na po yung pondo.

**[02:02:47.220] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001773)  
Kasi na-approve na po yung design no, yung central kitchen.

**[02:02:49.960] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001774)  
So, more than 200 central kitchen po ang i-build natin ngayon.

**[02:02:54.220] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001775)  
That will increase our provision of ano po?

**[02:02:57.200] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001776)  
Of hot meals.

**[02:02:59.720] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001777)  
So, lahat na po ay nag-iimplementa ng universal kinder, saka universal grade 1.

**[02:03:04.880] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001778)  
And then, including po yung mga pregnant mothers, pregnant learners,

**[02:03:08.880] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001779)  
saka yung young mothers also na nasa high school.

**[02:03:12.380] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001780)  
So, for the record, Asik Roger, lahat ng skulahan ng DepEd

**[02:03:16.900] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001781)  
nag-iimplement ng universal kinder at grade 1 ngayon.

**[02:03:19.940] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001782)  
Yes, po.

**[02:03:20.700] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001783)  
Yes.

**[02:03:22.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001784)  
And you did that from which months of this year?

**[02:03:26.320] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001785)  
Nag-start po yung...

**[02:03:27.200] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001786)  
Yung feeding po na by August or September.

**[02:03:30.600] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001787)  
Okay.

**[02:03:30.940] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001788)  
Nag-start po yung...

**[02:03:31.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001789)  
So, tama. It took some time to do the procurement.

**[02:03:34.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001790)  
It took some time kasi ang laki nga nung talo natin, di ba?

**[02:03:37.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001791)  
Pero as of today, we are successfully implementing this program.

**[02:03:42.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001792)  
For the record.

**[02:03:43.340] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001793)  
Yes, po.

**[02:03:44.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001794)  
Okay, congratulations.

**[02:03:45.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001795)  
Now, kung idadagdag po natin yung grade 2 at grade 3,

**[02:03:48.520] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001796)  
kasi yun po yung pinakalaman ng reforma,

**[02:03:51.960] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001797)  
K to grade 3 yung magkakaroon ng pagkain,

**[02:03:55.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001798)  
kakayanin ba?

**[02:03:57.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001799)  
ng DepEd at ng systems po ninyo

**[02:03:59.740] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001800)  
na hanggang grade 3 ay napupondohan po

**[02:04:02.000] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001801)  
at nabibigyan ng pagkain araw-araw?

**[02:04:05.000] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001802)  
Kaya po, pero mayroon lang po kaming request.

**[02:04:07.020] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001803)  
Sige po.

**[02:04:08.680] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001804)  
Kaya po, kailangan lang po namin ng tao

**[02:04:11.140] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001805)  
na mag-iimplement sa baba.

**[02:04:13.420] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001806)  
Kasi po, ayaw na naming ipasa sa teacher yung

**[02:04:16.800] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001807)  
pagmamanage ng feeding

**[02:04:18.500] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001808)  
dahil nag-i-increase na po yung capability natin

**[02:04:21.260] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001809)  
na magbigay ng hot meal

**[02:04:23.760] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001810)  
because we are building kitchens.

**[02:04:25.660] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001811)  
So, kailangan po natin ng mga...

**[02:04:27.200] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001812)  
additional manpower sa schools

**[02:04:29.480] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001813)  
para po ma-manage ng maayos po yung feeding program.

**[02:04:32.080] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001814)  
We can...

**[02:04:33.220] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001815)  
Anyway, madalas naman tayo nag-uhusap, Asik Roger.

**[02:04:35.440] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001816)  
We can talk offline

**[02:04:36.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001817)  
and maybe for the plenary,

**[02:04:38.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001818)  
we can have better numbers also.

**[02:04:41.020] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001819)  
Now, hindi naman lahat mag-centralize kitchen, di ba?

**[02:04:45.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001820)  
Tama ba ito na the larger percentage

**[02:04:47.360] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001821)  
will still have the nutritious food product

**[02:04:49.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001822)  
but for some areas that the LGU can handle it,

**[02:04:52.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001823)  
you will do the hot meals?

**[02:04:53.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001824)  
Yes, po.

**[02:04:54.080] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001825)  
Ano yung ratio niyan?

**[02:04:54.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001826)  
Ratio of areas or schools doing hot meals

**[02:05:01.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001827)  
and ratio of areas doing the nutritious food product?

**[02:05:09.180] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001828)  
Previously po, last year,

**[02:05:11.100] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001829)  
nasa 83% po yung purely nutritious food packs po.

**[02:05:14.780] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001830)  
Pero ngayon po, dahil marami ng kitchens,

**[02:05:17.640] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001831)  
bumaba na po yan substantially yung

**[02:05:19.600] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001832)  
nag-implement ng purely nutritious food packs.

**[02:05:23.380] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001833)  
Pero po, ang marami ngayon, Mr.

**[02:05:24.940] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001834)  
Chair, yung combination.

**[02:05:27.180] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001835)  
So, mayroon pong, marami pong school

**[02:05:29.020] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001836)  
na nag-implement ng

**[02:05:30.540] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001837)  
two days

**[02:05:32.400] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001838)  
nutritious food packs

**[02:05:34.940] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001839)  
and

**[02:05:35.880] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001840)  
three days na hot meals.

**[02:05:38.680] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001841)  
So, depende po sa strategy ng

**[02:05:40.760] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001842)  
school, binigyan po natin sila ng

**[02:05:42.860] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001843)  
leeway to strategize

**[02:05:45.140] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001844)  
how to implement yung combination po.

**[02:05:46.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001845)  
And whether hot meal siya or nutritious

**[02:05:49.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001846)  
food product siya, parehas pa rin yung presyo, no?

**[02:05:50.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001847)  
Yes, po.

**[02:05:52.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001848)  
The budget for last year was 25%.

**[02:05:54.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001849)  
Now, we want to increase it to 35 pesos.

**[02:05:58.260] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-001850)  
Sa gap po, is 25 pa rin.

**[02:06:00.160] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001851)  
Ah, okay.

**[02:06:00.980] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001852)  
So, major, major computation po ito, no?

**[02:06:04.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001853)  
So, please help us with the computation.

**[02:06:07.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001854)  
And in the same way na

**[02:06:09.860] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001855)  
the Senate President was Chairman of Finance at that time,

**[02:06:15.260] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001856)  
supported and pushed the increase in the classroom budget

**[02:06:19.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001857)  
and the school feeding budget,

**[02:06:21.440] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001858)  
we're hoping that we can do the same for the school

**[02:06:24.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001859)  
feeding budget this year.

**[02:06:26.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001860)  
And paniniwala po namin,

**[02:06:28.960] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001861)  
ito yung isa sa pinakamalaking reforma

**[02:06:31.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001862)  
na pwede natin gawin.

**[02:06:32.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001863)  
And if we're able to implement it well,

**[02:06:35.340] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001864)  
I expect that in three to six years,

**[02:06:38.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001865)  
our learning

**[02:06:39.000] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001866)  
gap problems

**[02:06:41.700] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001867)  
can be mitigated.

**[02:06:43.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001868)  
Do you agree, Secretary Sonny?

**[02:06:45.380] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001869)  
100 percent, Your Honor.

**[02:06:46.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001870)  
Thank you. Sige.

**[02:06:48.820] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001871)  
For my first round, although mahaba na itong first round ko,

**[02:06:51.080] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001872)  
on school safety, very quick lang.

**[02:06:53.620] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001873)  
Alam ho natin,

**[02:06:54.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001874)  
na hindi lang naman kagamitan

**[02:06:57.360] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001875)  
ang nagdudulot ng school safety.

**[02:06:59.340] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001876)  
In fact, if you look at the School Safety Act,

**[02:07:01.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001877)  
and Secretary Sani was kind enough to be here

**[02:07:04.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001878)  
when we sponsored it

**[02:07:05.880] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001879)  
here in the Senate,

**[02:07:08.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001880)  
ang laking bagay talaga dyan,

**[02:07:10.140] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001881)  
yung training ng mga teachers, parents,

**[02:07:12.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001882)  
and student leaders, diba?

**[02:07:14.620] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001883)  
So, are we already implementing that?

**[02:07:17.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001884)  
The training for the parents

**[02:07:20.700] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001885)  
and the teachers

**[02:07:21.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001886)  
and the student leaders

**[02:07:22.960] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001887)  
so they can identify yung mga parents,

**[02:07:24.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001888)  
yung mga possible problems.

**[02:07:26.860] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001889)  
Later on, we have our friends here from Stairway,

**[02:07:29.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001890)  
they can help us also,

**[02:07:30.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001891)  
but we'll tackle that later on.

**[02:07:32.180] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-001892)  
But currently, Secretary Sani, ginagawa na ba yan?

**[02:07:34.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001893)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:07:35.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001894)  
In fact, kaagapay,

**[02:07:36.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001895)  
we're being ably assisted by Stairway.

**[02:07:40.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001896)  
We're very thankful because

**[02:07:41.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001897)  
originally, yung intention ng kaagapay

**[02:07:43.680] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001898)  
sa academic performance, nakatungon

**[02:07:45.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001899)  
huyon, nakatingin huyon.

**[02:07:47.920] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001900)  
Pero because of recent events,

**[02:07:49.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001901)  
we realized it's the perfect vehicle

**[02:07:51.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001902)  
to do the

**[02:07:52.760] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001903)  
palakasin yung

**[02:07:54.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001904)  
pag-uusap, yung ugnayan

**[02:07:56.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001905)  
with the families

**[02:07:58.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001906)  
and the guardians, etc., Your Honor,

**[02:08:00.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-001907)  
para ma-realize that many of them

**[02:15:25.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002119)  
Beneficiaries.

**[02:15:26.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002120)  
Well, it's a shorter feeding cycle, Your Honor.

**[02:15:28.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002121)  
So, compared to the full school year, you're down to 120 days.

**[02:15:33.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002122)  
And the observation there is, very successful ka for 120 days,

**[02:15:38.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002123)  
but for the remaining days that you don't feed,

**[02:15:40.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002124)  
dun bumabalik sa lumang timbang o sa dating timbang yung bata.

**[02:15:44.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002125)  
Okay. I'd like that clarified.

**[02:15:48.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002126)  
So, we reduced it to 120.

**[02:15:53.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002127)  
For this year, it should be, it's 200, right?

**[02:15:56.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002128)  
It's the full school year, 200.

**[02:15:58.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002129)  
So, why did we reduce it?

**[02:16:00.920] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002130)  
Hindi po.

**[02:16:01.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002131)  
Yung proposal namin ho is for 200,

**[02:16:04.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002132)  
but I think it's just a procedure where really it's the NEP level that's restored.

**[02:16:10.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002133)  
And any congressional insertions,

**[02:16:12.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002134)  
alam po nila ni Sen Win as former chair of finance,

**[02:16:16.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002135)  
they just go back to the NEP level,

**[02:16:18.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002136)  
and there siguro ang hope there or ang expectation is that Congress will make the same insertion,

**[02:16:25.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002137)  
which may or may not happen, Your Honor.

**[02:16:27.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002138)  
Which?

**[02:16:28.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002139)  
Which places the burden on us when it should have been in the NEP.

**[02:16:36.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002140)  
Di ba?

**[02:16:36.520] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002141)  
Parang kaya nga namin ilagay, kasi nga yun ang kailangan eh.

**[02:16:40.580] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002142)  
Tapos babalik natin sa NEP.

**[02:16:42.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002143)  
Parang ano ba ito?

**[02:16:43.720] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002144)  
Parang, well, well, you know, they have the reasons.

**[02:16:47.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002145)  
Obviously, fiscal space is tight, et cetera.

**[02:16:50.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002146)  
But medyo malaking problema yung binaba mo, binalik mo sa 120 days

**[02:16:56.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002147)  
because based on your data,

**[02:16:58.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002148)  
parang talagang vacation drop, di ba?

**[02:17:06.460] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002149)  
Parang lahat mong ginawa mo, pinondohan mo, nawawala dahil kulang.

**[02:17:12.560] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002150)  
Di ba?

**[02:17:12.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002151)  
And that was already 200 days, no?

**[02:17:15.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002152)  
Your latest data was already at 200 days, right?

**[02:17:19.740] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002153)  
Is that correct?

**[02:17:21.180] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002154)  
O hindi pa?

**[02:17:22.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002155)  
180 yata last year, ho.

**[02:17:23.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002156)  
O yun, 180.

**[02:17:26.120] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002157)  
So, yun nga, miss.

**[02:17:28.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002158)  
Bumaba, tapos umakyat.

**[02:17:30.480] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002159)  
Di ba?

**[02:17:30.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002160)  
Where is that data?

**[02:17:31.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002161)  
What slide is that?

**[02:17:34.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002162)  
Ah, here.

**[02:17:35.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002163)  
42.53%, bumaba, 75, bumaba sa 33, from 24 to 25.

**[02:17:44.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002164)  
Tapos pagdating ng 25, 26, tumaas na naman, 47.

**[02:17:48.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002165)  
Di ba?

**[02:17:49.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002166)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:17:51.160] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002167)  
So, yung gap na yun, and this is 180 days, correct?

**[02:17:55.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002168)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:17:56.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002169)  
So, can you imagine if it's...

**[02:17:58.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002170)  
We'll go back to 120 days.

**[02:18:00.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002171)  
It's like, parang ano to?

**[02:18:04.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002172)  
Parang nakita na natin yung gap, nakita na natin bumabagsak pa rin at 180 days,

**[02:18:11.500] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002173)  
pero pinipilit natin yung 120, ah.

**[02:18:14.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002174)  
Parang ano ba ito?

**[02:18:15.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002175)  
Are we trying to solve the problem or are we just churning out numbers for the heck of it?

**[02:18:22.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002176)  
Di ba?

**[02:18:23.000] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002177)  
And for us to be able to put together, what?

**[02:18:27.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002178)  
Remedies.

**[02:18:28.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002179)  
But anyway, so obviously, we have to go back to 200 days.

**[02:18:33.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002180)  
I mean, that's the...

**[02:18:35.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002181)  
Kahit 180, I think, is adequate.

**[02:18:37.300] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002182)  
Kasi, Your Honor, sa umpisa ng school year, tinitimbang yun.

**[02:18:40.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002183)  
So, hindi ka nagpapakain sa day one, eh.

**[02:18:42.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002184)  
So, 180, I think, is more than enough.

**[02:18:45.260] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002185)  
Okay, okay.

**[02:18:46.300] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002186)  
Now, on the matter of your...

**[02:18:51.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002187)  
So, maybe you can prepare for plenary because I'll raise this again.

**[02:18:57.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002188)  
What is the impact of the reduction from 25 billion to 11 billion?

**[02:19:03.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002189)  
How will this...

**[02:19:04.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002190)  
You don't have to answer it now because...

**[02:19:06.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002191)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[02:19:07.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002192)  
That.

**[02:19:08.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002193)  
Plus, prepare for plenary, the question of...

**[02:19:13.760] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002194)  
Well, the Sagipsaka implementation because it turns out...

**[02:19:21.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002195)  
Maliit ang percentage, no?

**[02:19:23.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002196)  
Noong...

**[02:19:24.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002197)  
Where is that?

**[02:19:27.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002198)  
0.3% of your purchases for food in the school-based feeding program.

**[02:19:37.960] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002199)  
0.3% lang ang napunta doon sa Sagipsaka.

**[02:19:41.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002200)  
Yeah, 35.2 million, Your Honor.

**[02:19:43.380] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002201)  
Yes, of your 25 billion.

**[02:19:46.320] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002202)  
Of course, you know, it's staggered.

**[02:19:48.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002203)  
But why is it small?

**[02:19:51.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002204)  
What are the gaps?

**[02:19:52.920] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002205)  
What do you intend to do?

**[02:19:54.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002206)  
How do you intend to bring this forward?

**[02:19:57.660] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002207)  
And, again, in plenary, you can answer that.

**[02:20:01.580] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002208)  
But if you have initial comments or, you know...

**[02:20:06.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002209)  
With your permission, I'll ask Asik Roger to explain that.

**[02:20:10.040] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002210)  
Go ahead, sir.

**[02:20:11.800] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002211)  
Roger.

**[02:20:13.940] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002212)  
Yes, sir.

**[02:20:15.880] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002213)  
Bali po, five provinces and four provinces and one city po ang gumamit po ng modality ng procurement on Sagipsaka.

**[02:20:27.660] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002214)  
Which is the Cauyan City, Zamboanga del Sur, Sulu Province, General Santo City, and then Sorigao del Sur.

**[02:20:36.460] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002215)  
Amounting to 35.2 million po yung cost nung ginamit po sa Sagipsaka.

**[02:20:44.020] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002216)  
Which is very small. Medyo, ano po, mababa po talaga.

**[02:20:47.700] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002217)  
Yes.

**[02:20:47.940] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002218)  
Pero po, nagkandak na po kami ng mga orientation po sa ating mga school-based feeding coordinator sa buong bansa.

**[02:20:57.040] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002219)  
At...

**[02:20:57.540] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002220)  
We are trying to prepare, Mr. Chair, ng manual or guidelines on how to implement SBFP sa true Sagipsaka.

**[02:21:09.560] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002221)  
Para po, mas dumami po yung mag-implement nito.

**[02:21:14.460] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002222)  
And then, ang nakuha po naming information, Mr. Chair, yung mga individual farmers natin, small farmers,

**[02:21:23.660] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002223)  
ay medyo nahirapan mag-register po sa

**[02:21:27.540] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002224)  
FIDES system.

**[02:21:30.300] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002225)  
Kaya hindi po sila makasali sa procurement.

**[02:21:36.160] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002226)  
Kaya mas maganda po na ma-organize po talaga into cooperatives yung ating mga small farmers.

**[02:21:42.360] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002227)  
Para mayroon po silang scale na sumali sa...

**[02:21:46.640] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002228)  
Kasi po, pag nag-procure ka po ng feeding, it's the entire year na po.

**[02:21:51.640] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002229)  
Yes.

**[02:21:52.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002230)  
Kung hindi po consistent yung ani niya, parang mahirapan po siyang manalo doon sa...

**[02:21:57.540] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002231)  
sa procurement.

**[02:21:59.240] DepEd Assistant Secretary Roger B. Masapol** (asr-002232)  
So, kung ano po, kung organized po talaga sila, mas malaki po yung chances po, Mr. Chair.

**[02:22:04.340] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002233)  
Although, I think, well, maybe you can be flexible, no?

**[02:22:07.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002234)  
Kasi, before they get organized, it will take time.

**[02:22:11.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002235)  
Habang nandiyan yung produkto nila, nandiyan yung ani nila, na pag hindi binili, itatapon.

**[02:22:18.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002236)  
You know?

**[02:22:19.200] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002237)  
So, baka kailangan merong flexibility para...

**[02:22:22.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002238)  
As we work towards the ideal, because...

**[02:22:26.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002239)  
You know?

**[02:22:27.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002240)  
We've already passed here in the Senate yung Bureau of Agriculture and Fisheries Co-ops re-establishment, no?

**[02:22:34.060] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002241)  
So, that will take time.

**[02:22:35.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002242)  
So, maybe you can put...

**[02:22:37.280] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002243)  
And if you can submit to us yung sinasabing yung mahirap mag-register sa DA, no?

**[02:22:45.860] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002244)  
Because that shouldn't be a hurdle, no?

**[02:22:49.700] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002245)  
Dapat madali mag-register sa DA, no?

**[02:22:52.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002246)  
So, if you can give that to us, then we will work on that.

**[02:22:56.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002247)  
We'll do that, Your Honor.

**[02:22:57.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002248)  
Yes, and yeah, I hope.

**[02:23:00.820] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002249)  
And I'd like to think, in fairness, the implementation of the Sagipsaka program for your feeding program,

**[02:23:08.100] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002250)  
dahil nag-umpisa ang pasokan ng August, doon lang, by and large, di ba, yung pag-iimplementa,

**[02:23:15.880] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002251)  
nag-umpisa rin about a couple of months ago, three months ago.

**[02:23:19.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002252)  
And so, you're really still in the initial stages of being able to implement the program and the law.

**[02:23:27.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002253)  
So, we'll grant you that.

**[02:23:29.980] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002254)  
But we'd like to, during plenary, just submit to us, how do you intend to fully implement this?

**[02:23:36.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002255)  
What are the challenges?

**[02:23:38.400] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002256)  
What do we have to address on the supply side?

**[02:23:42.780] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002257)  
The demand side, walang problema eh.

**[02:23:45.440] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002258)  
Yung supply side, I'm sure you're also going to have a lot of challenges there.

**[02:23:49.680] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002259)  
And we'd like to address both.

**[02:23:52.360] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002260)  
In the end, you're going to have fresh produce.

**[02:23:57.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002261)  
Sariwang pagkain, you'd like to move away from the, what is the term?

**[02:24:02.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002262)  
NFP to hot meals, di ba?

**[02:24:07.640] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002263)  
And I know for a fact that many of your school children look forward to the hot meals.

**[02:24:14.140] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002264)  
It's a plus, an incentive for them to go to school.

**[02:24:19.620] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002265)  
And therefore, moving towards that direction will not just ensure better quality.

**[02:24:27.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002266)  
That quality education for our children, it will also help our farmers and our fisher folks.

**[02:24:32.900] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002267)  
So, you're really having one fund, but two beneficiaries.

**[02:24:39.200] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002268)  
Which is how government spending should be, di ba?

**[02:24:42.840] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002269)  
One expenditure, but several outcomes and several outcomes, yes.

**[02:24:53.600] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002270)  
So, yun na muna. My time is up.

**[02:24:56.240] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002271)  
I will...

**[02:24:57.540] Senator Francis Kiko Pangilinan** (asr-002272)  
I will work on my second round, sir.

**[02:24:59.240] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-002273)  
Thank you, Sen. Kiko.

**[02:25:00.860] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-002274)  
Secretary Angara is asking for a break.

**[02:25:03.880] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-002275)  
So, you can come back soon.

**[02:25:05.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002276)  
Back to room liquidity break.

**[02:25:06.660] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-002277)  
Yes, go ahead, go ahead, sir.

**[02:25:08.020] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002278)  
Thank you.

**[02:25:09.440] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-002279)  
Sen. Allen has said that he will wait for Secretary Angara to come back.

**[02:25:13.340] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-002280)  
Sen. Tulfo, do you have questions for other members of the DepEd family?

**[02:25:18.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-002281)  
You may wish to go, or would you want to wait for Secretary Angara?

**[02:25:20.760] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002282)  
Yeah, I want to ask a few questions now, Mr. Chair.

**[02:25:24.620] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-002283)  
Yeah, go ahead, go ahead.

**[02:25:25.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002284)  
I know some of the...

**[02:25:27.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002285)  
Usecs may be able to answer this question.

**[02:25:31.480] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002286)  
I don't know which one, pero my question is, ano po ba talaga itong aral program?

**[02:25:36.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002287)  
15.32 billion po ang request po ninyo.

**[02:25:40.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002288)  
And zero po sa net.

**[02:25:44.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002289)  
I don't understand, bakit ginawang zero po ito?

**[02:25:48.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002290)  
Pero ang gab naman, naawa pa ng konti, nilagay ng 350 million, nagbibigay ng bariya.

**[02:25:55.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002291)  
Okay, can somebody explain this to us?

**[02:25:57.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002292)  
What is aral program? How important is this?

**[02:26:00.920] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-002293)  
Yes, is it Usec or Oracion?

**[02:26:02.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-002294)  
Usec Oracion, go ahead, please.

**[02:26:06.320] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002295)  
Good afternoon, po, Mr. Chair and Sen. Tulfo.

**[02:26:10.400] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002296)  
The aral program po is a remediation program.

**[02:26:14.740] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002297)  
It comes from the aral law.

**[02:26:17.880] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002298)  
We know from international assessments and from national assessments

**[02:26:22.880] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002299)  
that we have been doing very poorly over the years.

**[02:26:27.540] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002300)  
And so it's very timely that the aral law was passed.

**[02:26:34.500] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002301)  
And so now we have a clear basis for a program that will provide remediation

**[02:26:42.760] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002302)  
for reading, for mathematics, and for science, po.

**[02:26:47.440] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002303)  
Unfortunately, the law is unfunded.

**[02:26:51.380] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002304)  
And we have implemented it.

**[02:26:55.780] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002305)  
We, last year,

**[02:26:57.540] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002306)  
we implemented it for the first time.

**[02:26:59.780] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002307)  
We did aral reading, po.

**[02:27:01.980] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002308)  
And then we continued on in summer.

**[02:27:05.240] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002309)  
And then this school year, po, we're doing reading or literacy and mathematics.

**[02:27:11.280] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002310)  
And then we are also already looking into how to do it in science.

**[02:27:17.860] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002311)  
The gains have been explained through our video, po.

**[02:27:21.940] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002312)  
For example, out of the 6.7 million,

**[02:27:26.680] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002313)  
struggling readers on the first year of implementation

**[02:27:31.060] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002314)  
by the end of the school year,

**[02:27:32.740] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002315)  
that went down to 2.2.

**[02:27:35.000] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002316)  
And then the number of learners, naman, po,

**[02:27:38.560] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002317)  
who are able to read at grade level increased.

**[02:27:42.540] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002318)  
And aside from our own metrics, po,

**[02:27:45.580] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002319)  
we engaged the youth impact,

**[02:27:47.820] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002320)  
and that was also presented a while ago.

**[02:27:50.940] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002321)  
And so that's it, po.

**[02:27:53.180] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002322)  
That's its importance.

**[02:27:55.220] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002323)  
It's the mainstream.

**[02:27:56.680] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002324)  
It's the strategy by which we can improve

**[02:27:59.900] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002325)  
the outcomes for our learners,

**[02:28:02.600] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002326)  
specifically those who are not performing

**[02:28:06.060] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002327)  
at expected levels in the grade level.

**[02:28:09.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002328)  
How do you do that, Yusef?

**[02:28:10.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002329)  
Is it after school hours?

**[02:28:13.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002330)  
Parang tutoring?

**[02:28:15.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002331)  
Tama po ba?

**[02:28:15.900] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002332)  
This is a tutorial model, po.

**[02:28:18.260] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002333)  
And therefore, a teacher handles from 10 to 15 students,

**[02:28:23.440] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002334)  
preferably 10.

**[02:28:25.280] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002335)  
The schools are left,

**[02:28:26.680] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002336)  
to identify the best modality

**[02:28:29.500] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002337)  
for the conduct of the session.

**[02:28:34.220] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002338)  
Some do it before class hours.

**[02:28:36.840] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002339)  
Others do it after class hours.

**[02:28:39.660] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002340)  
Others, other schools do it during breaks.

**[02:28:43.800] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002341)  
And then there are also schools

**[02:28:45.300] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002342)  
that need to do it on Saturdays,

**[02:28:48.500] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-002343)  
two hours on Saturdays, po.

**[02:28:50.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-002344)

**[02:42:09.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002747)  
that,

**[02:42:10.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002748)

**[02:42:13.700] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002749)

**[02:42:17.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002750)  
we have approximately

**[02:42:22.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002751)

**[02:42:24.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002752)  
To throw everything into education,

**[02:42:26.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002753)  
and then, of course,

**[02:42:27.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002754)

**[02:42:35.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002757)  
Can you show this slide,

**[02:42:36.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002758)  
yung the difference between Philippine Science and DepEd?

**[02:42:49.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002759)  
Yung what's the difference?

**[02:42:56.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002760)  
Sorry.

**[02:43:08.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002761)  
I'm sorry, Sek,

**[02:43:08.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002762)  
medyo malayo yung screen sa inyo.

**[02:43:10.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002763)  
But, just going through it, no?

**[02:43:13.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002764)  
So, 2.4 billion

**[02:43:15.500] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002765)  
ang budget nila.

**[02:43:18.460] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002766)  
2025 pa to.

**[02:43:19.860] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002767)  
So, 734 billion

**[02:43:21.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002768)  
and DepEd.

**[02:43:21.980] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002769)  
But, of course,

**[02:43:23.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002770)  
they only have 10,000 students,

**[02:43:25.860] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002771)  
almost 11, you have 21 million students.

**[02:43:30.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002772)  
But what's significant for me here is three points.

**[02:43:33.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002773)  
Point number one, we're spending more or less

**[02:43:37.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002774)  
34,690 per student sa DepEd,

**[02:43:41.260] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002775)  
but we're spending 224,000 per student

**[02:43:45.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002776)  
sa Philippine Science High School.

**[02:43:48.460] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002777)  
So every time someone says now that Philippines

**[02:43:51.460] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002778)  
is number 7, number 8, number 9, Malayo, etc.,

**[02:43:56.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002779)  
when you talk about the Philippines,

**[02:43:57.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002780)  
you talk about many things,

**[02:43:58.740] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002781)  
but basically you're talking about the people.

**[02:44:01.560] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002782)  
So if we can somehow get the spending

**[02:44:04.460] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002783)  
closer to what we're doing with Philippines High School

**[02:44:07.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002784)  
rather than what we're doing with our public schools now,

**[02:44:11.660] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002785)  
then we will have a better Philippines.

**[02:44:14.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002786)  
Rather than talk politics,

**[02:44:16.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002787)  
sinong leader, sinong dapat,

**[02:44:18.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002788)  
paano mababago yung Pilipinas,

**[02:44:20.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002789)  
simple lang.

**[02:44:22.460] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002790)  
How do we spend more,

**[02:44:25.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002791)  
but spend smaller?

**[02:44:25.860] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002792)  
Smarter.

**[02:44:26.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002793)  
Okay.

**[02:44:27.240] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002794)  
But the second point I want to make,

**[02:44:28.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002795)  
Mr. Secretary,

**[02:44:30.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002796)  
classroom size,

**[02:44:31.660] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002797)  
30 is to 1 lang yung science high school,

**[02:44:35.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002798)  
70 to 100 ang DepEd.

**[02:44:38.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002799)  
So science naman,

**[02:44:41.020] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002800)  
yung learning

**[02:44:42.700] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002801)  
dun sa DepEd side.

**[02:44:48.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002802)  
And the third point I want to make is

**[02:44:50.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002803)  
single shift, 730 to 415.

**[02:44:52.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002804)  
I always use 7 to 3,

**[02:44:55.500] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002805)  
or 8 to 4,

**[02:44:56.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002806)  
kasi nung elementary high school ako,

**[02:44:58.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002807)  
um,

**[02:45:00.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002808)  
and the schools near us, sa south kami, BF Paranaque, Alabang area,

**[02:45:06.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002809)  
it was either 7 to 3 or 8 to 4.

**[02:45:10.380] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002810)  
But if you look at DepEd, it's 6 to 12, then 12 to 6.45.

**[02:45:15.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002811)  
Pero pag whole day, what is it?

**[02:45:18.740] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002812)  
Ganon din, mga 6 hours din.

**[02:45:20.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002813)  
8 to 3, mga ganon. 8 to 2, 8 to 3.

**[02:45:24.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002814)  
But sa memo ngayon, it's 5.5 hours lang eh.

**[02:45:30.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002815)  
We're trying to prepare for El Nino now, Your Honor.

**[02:45:33.020] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002816)  
So we're providing flexibility, yung hot hours na 10, 11 to 2.

**[02:45:38.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002817)  
The school is free to reschedule their classes around it, Your Honor.

**[02:45:41.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002818)  
I'll go back to that, but the main point of my questions and interpolation is that

**[02:45:46.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002819)  
quality of education, and whether you talk about mental health,

**[02:45:51.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002820)  
whether you talk about teenage pregnancy,

**[02:45:54.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002821)  
whether you talk about school shooting,

**[02:45:58.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002822)  
quality time with their teachers is important.

**[02:46:03.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002823)  
So kung dati tayong 8 hours sa school, ngayon 5.5 hours na lang.

**[02:46:08.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002824)  
Kung dati tayong 40 sa isang classroom, it hasn't been 40 in maybe 20 years,

**[02:46:13.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002825)  
but I'm talking before that, no?

**[02:46:16.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002826)  
Ngayon, 50, 60, 70.

**[02:46:19.060] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002827)  
Kung dati may recess at lunch,

**[02:46:21.040] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002828)  
so studies show that not only should you have,

**[02:46:24.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002829)  
30-minute lunch, it should be a sit-down lunch.

**[02:46:27.480] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002830)  
So even if we provide the food and it's nutritious,

**[02:46:30.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002831)  
if they take it with them and they don't sit down for at least 20 minutes,

**[02:46:36.460] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002832)  
yung both the nutritional and also the fellowship and the, you know, being with students,

**[02:46:43.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002833)  
apektado talaga ito, no?

**[02:46:45.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002834)  
So I'll go back to that.

**[02:46:47.300] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002835)  
Quick question.

**[02:46:48.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002836)  
Some changes when you were still in EDCOM, yung cluster sa kabinete,

**[02:46:53.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002837)  
between the TESDA,

**[02:46:54.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002838)  
CHED and DepEd, how's that going?

**[02:46:58.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002839)  
Very good, Your Honor, because the President created an education cluster

**[02:47:02.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002840)  
based on the recommendation of EDCOM,

**[02:47:06.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002841)  
and it's called the Education Workforce Development Group, Your Honor,

**[02:47:11.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002842)  
headed by Asec Villafuerte.

**[02:47:16.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002843)  
It comprises eight agencies, actually.

**[02:47:19.800] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002844)  
It's an expanded education cluster because it's not only the demand,

**[02:47:24.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002845)  
the demand side of education, which is the graduates and what careers they will enter,

**[02:47:28.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002846)  
but also the supply side.

**[02:47:30.160] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002847)  
So kasama po namin yung Department of Labor, yung Department of Migrant Workers,

**[02:47:34.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002848)  
ang DepDev, and some cabinet agencies want to join us, like DOST,

**[02:47:40.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002849)  
and you mentioned Pisay, Your Honor, and I couldn't agree with you more.

**[02:47:43.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002850)  
That is definitely the aspiration, Your Honor.

**[02:47:45.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002851)  
And in fairness, you know, in the time that Senpia and yourself were chairs of the Education Committee,

**[02:47:50.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002852)  
I've seen so much improvement because yung 34,000 per capita,

**[02:47:54.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002853)  
dati parang 30,000 lang yun, or 29,000, 27,000.

**[02:47:58.760] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002854)  
So kahit papano, it's incremental, but I think all these things have made a difference, Your Honor.

**[02:48:05.300] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002855)  
And really, it's a steep climb, but it has become more manageable now because of all the changes.

**[02:48:11.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002856)  
We're building on what previous generations and previous leaders built upon.

**[02:48:16.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002857)  
So yung, definitely I've seen the figures increase.

**[02:48:20.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002858)  
Like yung MOOE pa lang, nakita ko natin eh.

**[02:48:24.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002859)  
Yung formula advocated by EDCOM and ADB.

**[02:48:27.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002860)  
So talagang mas maraming factors were taken into account na talaga.

**[02:48:30.440] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002861)  
So it's no longer so one-sided in favor of Pisay.

**[02:48:34.060] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002862)  
I think the aspiration is there, although malayo pa.

**[02:48:38.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002863)  
Many of the things that Pisay has, like the computers, the tutorials, the attention to special needs students,

**[02:48:47.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002864)  
I think we're trying to make our way there, Your Honor.

**[02:48:52.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002865)  
So first, let me say,

**[02:48:54.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002866)  
I agree with you.

**[02:48:55.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002867)  
There's so much innovation, so much care, so much effort, so much change.

**[02:49:00.700] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002868)  
As I said at the start of the hearing,

**[02:49:03.840] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002869)  
pasensya ka na kung mas maraming reklamo at anong problema.

**[02:49:07.700] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002870)  
Just because we're trying to solve it, but that doesn't mean we do not appreciate everything.

**[02:49:10.860] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002871)  
We understand, Your Honor.

**[02:49:11.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002872)  
Maybe the main point I wanted to make, that's why I put the Pisay,

**[02:49:14.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002873)  
because I am only one of the two senators who oppose the K-12.

**[02:49:21.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002874)  
And I showed this chart then.

**[02:49:24.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002875)  
But it was much different, and this was the difference.

**[02:49:28.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002876)  
Then, at that time, pre-K-12, Pisay already had a semi-K-12 schedule.

**[02:49:38.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002877)  
So ang nadagdag lang sa Pisay, doong first two years noong K-12, was free time.

**[02:49:44.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002878)  
Because they already had what it was without the additional two years.

**[02:49:49.300] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002879)  
But when DEPED added another two years, so my point, Mr. Secretary,

**[02:49:54.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002880)  
if you take out the two years now, I'm not saying that there won't be a problem,

**[02:49:59.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002881)  
but your per capita and many of your problems,

**[02:50:01.720] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002882)  
so let me put it another way, we're looking for a breakthrough.

**[02:50:05.020] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002883)  
So definitely, just the discussion on the feeding, the internet, yung kuryenta, yung security,

**[02:50:13.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002884)  
they're making a big, big difference.

**[02:50:16.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002885)  
I'm really talking about more of breakthrough.

**[02:50:19.240] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002886)  
But the reason I ask about the cluster is kasi yung original,

**[02:50:24.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002887)  
or MECS or DECS, Ministry of Education, Sports and Culture,

**[02:50:30.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002888)  
sa cluster ba na yun kasama ang culture at saka sports?

**[02:50:33.760] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002889)  
Hindi po namin sila kasama, pero kasama po namin sila sa TEC.

**[02:50:37.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002890)  
Yung NCCA is part of the Teacher Education Council.

**[02:50:40.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002891)  
Since you are one of the most influential voices in education,

**[02:50:45.300] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002892)  
and in the cabinet, I hope.

**[02:50:47.800] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002893)  
I was looking kasi at Director Barnes, and may binulong ako about art.

**[02:50:54.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002894)  
And my staff brought it up to me, and I said, thank you for reminding me.

**[02:50:59.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002895)  
And E.D. Carroll, and don't get mad at him.

**[02:51:02.380] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002896)  
Because when we started to, I say we because we were either congressmen or senators noon,

**[02:51:10.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002897)  
when we started to strip down DepEd, we kept on saying, doon na lang muna tayo sa basic.

**[02:51:16.260] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002898)  
So nag-umpisa doon sa reading and writing muna, kasi nga wala nang baseball field, wala nang football field,

**[02:51:22.740] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002899)  
pati yung kung saan nag-a...

**[02:51:24.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002900)  
Monday flag ceremony, yung iba wala nang space, yung dating mga open space na garden,

**[02:51:32.860] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002901)  
tinayuan na yung building, yung dating covert court, building na rin ngayon.

**[02:51:37.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002902)  
Yung iba naman na buildings, meron sa loob na mga additional facilities.

**[02:51:41.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002903)  
And then, after that, ang sinabi, yung PE, strip down na rin natin, and then STEM.

**[02:51:49.980] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002904)  
Ang importante naman sa lahat is science,

**[02:51:54.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002905)  
technology, math, language, whether Filipino, English.

**[02:52:01.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002906)  
But then, after 20 years, we realized that hindi pwedeng pa na yung academics,

**[02:52:05.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002907)  
yung parang, kung baga sa atleta, what about the soul?

**[02:52:20.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002910)

**[02:52:22.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002911)  
Martial arts,

**[02:52:24.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002912)

**[02:52:28.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002913)  
that's all part of it, eh.

**[02:52:30.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002914)

**[02:52:44.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002919)

**[02:52:54.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002922)

**[02:53:04.380] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002925)

**[02:53:07.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002926)

**[02:53:12.880] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002927)  
So she did, kung baga sa politiko na house-to-house,

**[02:53:16.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002928)  
she did a school-to-school survey and they called it Project Tao during that time.

**[02:53:21.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002929)  
Parang tinao-tao nila yung schools.

**[02:53:24.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002930)  
Ngayon, your database, your AI, it's much better,

**[02:53:29.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002931)  
but if we go down to the district level, would you have that information?

**[02:53:33.980] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002932)

**[02:53:36.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002933)  
Yes, Your Honor, we have that data now.

**[02:53:39.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002934)  
That's one of our goals when we came into the department in 2024,

**[02:53:42.340] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002935)  
to really substantially improve the database

**[02:53:44.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002936)  
because much data at the school level is not brought up to the division level.

**[02:53:49.380] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-002937)  
It's not brought up to the central office level.

**[02:53:53.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002938)  
So may I ask?

**[02:53:54.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002939)  
May I ask your permission that we start the debate today whether or not we keep K-12 the next five years

**[02:54:02.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002940)

**[02:54:04.700] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002941)

**[02:54:13.460] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002943)

**[02:54:17.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002944)

**[02:54:22.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002945)

**[02:54:24.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002946)  
We'll have a music room, et cetera.

**[02:54:26.260] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002947)  
Then we do a score of our schools.

**[02:54:29.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002948)  
Bala ka na kung per region, per congressional district and everything.

**[02:54:32.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002949)  
Ang point ko lang, Sek, di ba?

**[02:54:36.860] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002950)

**[02:54:41.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002951)  
you start with an inventory, di ba?

**[02:54:43.680] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002952)  
So if we're going to have a debate how to improve the education system,

**[02:54:48.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002953)  
it's not much of a debate yung pinag-uusapan natin ngayon.

**[02:54:50.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002954)

**[02:54:54.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002955)

**[02:54:56.160] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002956)

**[02:55:06.060] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002959)

**[02:55:09.380] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002960)

**[02:55:30.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002964)

**[02:55:36.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002965)

**[02:55:41.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002966)  
ang tinuturo sa'yo to be competitive, of course, to be honest, to do the hard work.

**[02:55:45.740] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002967)

**[02:55:50.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002968)

**[02:55:53.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002969)  
Wala.

**[02:55:54.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002970)

**[02:55:56.460] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002971)

**[02:55:59.860] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002972)  
Creativity.

**[02:56:01.040] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002973)  
So on that one point lang, may you allow me to also lobby kay Director Barnes.

**[02:56:21.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002977)  
Taga DFA yung dating nandun at sinabi niya sa akin,

**[02:56:24.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002978)  
Sir, yung dati mong nilagyan ng pondo na soft diplomacy, wala na?

**[02:56:29.300] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002979)

**[02:56:34.360] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002980)

**[02:56:44.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002982)  
Di ba?

**[02:56:44.560] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002983)

**[02:56:46.800] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002984)

**[02:56:50.880] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002985)  
So, we were hoping at one point in time that,

**[02:56:53.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002986)

**[02:56:56.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002987)

**[02:56:59.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002988)

**[02:57:03.920] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002989)

**[02:57:18.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002992)

**[02:57:20.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002993)

**[02:57:21.300] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002994)  
Let's see.

**[02:57:22.060] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002995)  
Paxilica.

**[02:57:22.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002996)  
AI.

**[02:57:23.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002997)  
But one thing, it's very obvious.

**[02:57:27.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002998)  
If you look at the very few artists who made it big in the world,

**[02:57:33.020] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-002999)

**[02:57:37.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003000)

**[02:57:41.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003001)

**[02:57:47.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003002)

**[02:57:48.520] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003003)  
The beauty about art is you don't need to manufacture

**[02:57:52.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003004)

**[02:57:54.500] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003005)

**[02:57:57.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003006)  
It's the talent ng artist.

**[02:58:00.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003007)

**[02:58:04.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003008)

**[02:58:07.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003009)  
So, for example, of course, I'd hope we'd be the jewelry center of the world.

**[02:58:13.880] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003010)

**[02:58:16.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003011)

**[02:58:18.240] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003012)  
you still have to import diamonds.

**[02:58:21.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003013)  
You still have to have the artisan.

**[02:58:22.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003014)

**[02:58:24.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003015)  
But for artists, it's really giving them a break abroad, eh.

**[02:58:30.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003016)  
So, I hope the National Museum and I don't know who pa,

**[02:58:33.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003017)

**[02:58:35.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003018)  
Ang dami natin aspiring artists.

**[02:58:38.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003019)  
Breakthrough lang.

**[02:58:40.300] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003020)  
And then, really, someone to champion it.

**[02:58:42.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003021)  
Kasi, like, Sec Sonny yung sabi sa akin nung isang artist,

**[02:58:45.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003022)  
Sir, yung permit namin palabas para sa show abroad,

**[02:58:49.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003023)  
iba pa daw yun, sabi ng costume, sa permit pa uwi.

**[02:58:52.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003024)  
So, these are small things, but it's barriers, eh.

**[02:58:56.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003025)  
Diba?

**[02:58:57.440] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003026)  
And then, pagka sikat na sikat na,

**[02:58:59.680] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003027)  
I'll give you an example.

**[02:59:00.700] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003028)  
Let's say, si Ronald Ventura, diba?

**[02:59:03.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003029)  
Who doesn't want to either know him or appreciate his painting

**[02:59:07.040] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003030)  
or have one of his painting

**[02:59:08.800] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003031)  
or kung meron siyang show abroad, sama na ako.

**[02:59:11.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003032)  
But what did it take before he got there?

**[02:59:14.540] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003033)  
Diba?

**[02:59:14.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003034)  
So, there's so much we can do.

**[02:59:16.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003035)  
So, allow me lang to use half of my five minutes

**[02:59:20.100] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003036)  
to just lobby in that.

**[02:59:21.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003037)  
And you,

**[02:59:22.940] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003038)  
your father, Senator Angara,

**[02:59:24.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003039)

**[02:59:27.560] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003040)

**[02:59:32.020] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003042)

**[02:59:33.280] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003043)

**[02:59:34.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003044)

**[02:59:36.560] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003045)

We'll work on it, Mr. Chair.

**[02:59:41.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003047)

**[02:59:50.080] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003050)  
The likes of Patis Tesoro, Manny Gariba,

**[02:59:52.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003051)  
et cetera.

**[02:59:53.760] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003052)  
So, I'll link that now with the TVL.

And the senior high school tech pro,

one of the promises of K-12 was that employable.

**[03:00:09.400] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003055)

**[03:00:11.040] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003056)  
And then let me link it with TESDA.

**[03:00:12.700] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003057)  
That's why I asked about your cluster.

When we passed the K-12,

**[03:00:19.720] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003059)  
you were still in the house.

**[03:00:20.980] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003060)
