**[03:00:24.060] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003061)  
hindi mo na kailangan mag-out-of-school youth.

**[03:00:27.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003062)  
Kasi, at malamang,

**[03:00:29.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003063)  
hindi mo na kailangang mag-college unless gusto mo.

**[03:00:32.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003064)  
Kasi pag-graduate mo, employable ka na.

**[03:00:35.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003065)  
But if you look at the last 2013, 2014, 12 years,

**[03:00:40.900] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003066)

**[03:00:43.220] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003067)  
which is good.

**[03:00:44.820] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003068)

**[03:00:46.580] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003069)  
So instead of graduating senior high school,

**[03:00:51.600] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003070)

**[03:00:54.200] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003071)  
Because TESDA is working through the schools

**[03:00:57.000] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003072)  
and through the...

**[03:00:59.120] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003073)  
tracks.

**[03:01:01.340] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003074)  
Parang humiwalay completely.

**[03:01:04.080] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003075)  
And two years ago,

**[03:01:05.780] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003076)  
I think, Senator Joel, di ba,

**[03:01:07.640] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003077)  
when you were chairman,

**[03:01:08.720] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003078)

**[03:01:12.620] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003080)

**[03:01:14.960] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003081)  
at pati yung, what you call this,

**[03:01:17.180] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003082)  
yung mag-accredit and trainers.

**[03:01:19.140] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003083)

**[03:01:20.980] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003084)  
So now, DepEd has TechPro?

Correct?

**[03:01:25.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003086)  
That's correct, Mr.

**[03:01:26.620] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003087)  
It's just a change in nomenclature.

**[03:01:28.480] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003088)  
But it's also,

**[03:01:29.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003089)  
I think the goal is really to change attitudes

**[03:01:32.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003090)  
towards technical and vocational education.

**[03:01:35.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003091)  
Because there's a cultural bias against it.

**[03:01:38.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003092)  
And you mentioned employment.

**[03:01:40.200] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003093)  
The biggest number of unemployed

**[03:01:41.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003094)

**[03:01:43.340] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003095)  
So that's why that should factor into the debate.

**[03:01:46.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003096)  
Of course, it's not just an education question.

**[03:01:48.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-003097)  
It's an economy and industrial policy question.

**[03:01:51.420] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003098)  
I'm happy you brought that up

**[03:01:52.660] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003099)  
because that's, in fact,

**[03:01:55.320] Senator Alan Peter S. Cayetano** (asr-003100)  
an argument to spend more for...

**[03:58:03.660] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004865)

**[03:58:06.300] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004866)

**[03:58:10.580] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004868)

**[03:58:12.540] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004869)

**[03:58:14.200] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004870)

**[03:58:16.140] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004871)

**[03:58:18.220] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004872)

**[03:58:20.140] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004873)

**[03:58:22.140] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004874)

**[03:58:24.060] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004875)

**[03:58:26.220] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004876)

**[03:58:27.840] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-004877)  
Quick intervention. Could you tell us,

**[03:58:29.900] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-004878)

**[03:58:31.400] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-004879)  
considering in the previous years,

**[03:58:34.060] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-004880)  
we had problems here?

**[03:58:35.980] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-004881)  
Ano po yung binago ninyo sa inyong sistema

**[03:58:38.000] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-004882)  
kaya umabot po tayo dito?

**[03:58:41.360] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004883)  
The

**[03:58:41.840] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004884)  
Secretary had really directed

**[03:58:44.340] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004885)  
us to shorten

**[03:58:46.200] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004886)  
the procurement period

**[03:58:48.100] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004887)  
and we were able to,

**[03:58:50.140] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004888)  
to do that. In the past,

**[03:58:52.320] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004889)  
we would begin by

**[03:58:53.780] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004890)  
developing the textbooks and that

**[03:58:55.880] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004891)  
took so long. And

**[03:58:57.640] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004892)  
we understand that in the

**[03:59:00.080] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004893)  
past, it would take three, four,

**[03:59:02.220] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004894)  
five years to develop a

**[03:59:04.040] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004895)  
textbook. So until delivery time,

**[03:59:06.200] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004896)  
that would be years.

**[03:59:09.640] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004897)  
So

**[03:59:09.960] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004898)  
yun po, the procurement

**[03:59:11.820] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004899)  
process had been shortened

**[03:59:13.480] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004900)  
significantly. We would also

**[03:59:16.080] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004901)  
like to express at this point

**[03:59:18.180] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004902)  
that in the previous ten

**[03:59:20.060] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004903)  
years, we were only able

**[03:59:21.860] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004904)  
to procure 27 out of

**[03:59:24.000] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004905)  
88 titles. And then

**[03:59:26.040] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004906)  
in school years,

**[03:59:27.640] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004907)  
in 2024 and 2025,

**[03:59:29.960] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004908)  
60 out of 105 titles.

**[03:59:31.900] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004909)  
But for the first time, we really

**[03:59:33.780] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004910)  
succeeded to procure all

**[03:59:36.000] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004911)  
105 titles

**[03:59:38.140] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004912)  
this year. And as I

**[03:59:39.920] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004913)  
mentioned, deliveries will go on

**[03:59:41.860] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004914)  
until December. Because before,

**[03:59:44.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-004915)  
we were trying to create

**[03:59:45.780] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-004916)  
new textbooks every

**[03:59:48.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-004917)  
year. Well now, we just...

**[03:59:50.060] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-004918)  
Tama po ba? We chose

**[03:59:52.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-004919)  
the available in the market and we bought

**[03:59:54.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004920)  
that?

**[03:59:54.600] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004920-split-2)  
Yes po.

**[03:59:55.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004920-split-3)  
Okay.

**[03:59:57.960] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004922)

**[04:00:00.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004923)

**[04:00:05.580] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004925)

**[04:00:10.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004927)

**[04:00:10.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004928)

**[04:00:11.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004929)  
Existing policy and then the list will expand na lang.

**[04:00:14.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004930)  
If we've already accredited the 150 books, then you're accredited already.

**[04:00:18.360] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-004931)  
Congratulations for a simple policy shift that yielded such great results.

**[04:00:23.280] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004932)

**[04:00:28.300] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004933)  
And then yung process of developing the manuscript would take one year.

**[04:00:32.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004934)  
The process of printing would take another year.

**[04:00:34.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004935)  
Then the delivery would take another year.

**[04:00:36.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004936)  
So rather than yung printing and delivery, that's already one contract pinagsama na ho namin.

**[04:00:42.040] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004937)

**[04:00:44.740] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004938)  
Hindi na sineparate yung contract.

**[04:00:46.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004939)  
And then yung developing of the manuscript, it's no longer an exclusive dep-ed prerogative, Your Honor.

**[04:00:51.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004940)  
It can be pre-accredited books.

**[04:00:53.300] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004941)  
It can be the books bought by the private schools.

**[04:00:55.380] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004942)  
The universe is open, basically, to whoever.

**[04:00:58.300] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004943)  
However, books are of a sufficient quality, Your Honor.

**[04:01:02.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004944)  
Dati kasi manuscript, if I'm not mistaken, pinapabid ba yun?

**[04:01:09.200] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004945)

**[04:01:11.340] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004946)

**[04:01:15.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004947)  
So that really, early procurement, because we have a very good procurement specialist, Yusec Ruiz.

**[04:01:21.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004948)

**[04:01:24.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004949)  
That's why nakompleto na ho namin yung 100.

**[04:01:28.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004950)  
So from start to end, normally, three years yan.

**[04:01:31.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004951)

**[04:01:36.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004952)

**[04:01:38.380] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004953)

**[04:01:40.540] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004955)

**[04:01:41.140] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004956)

**[04:01:42.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004957)

**[04:01:44.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004958)

**[04:01:49.920] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004959)

**[04:01:52.880] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004960)

**[04:01:58.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004961)

**[04:02:00.400] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-004962)  
That's correct, Mr. President.

**[04:02:01.160] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004963)  
So right now, narinig ko kay Yusec, Mel, on the average, we're at around 70%.

**[04:02:12.760] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004965)

**[04:02:22.300] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004967-split-3)

**[04:02:23.120] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004968)

**[04:02:24.240] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004969)  
Because ang next question ko is,

**[04:02:28.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004970)

**[04:02:45.920] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004974)

**[04:02:48.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004975)

**[04:02:52.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004976)

**[04:02:54.600] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004977)

**[04:02:58.300] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004978)  
105 titles this year,

**[04:03:01.120] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004979)

**[04:03:10.180] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004981)

**[04:03:15.440] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004983)

**[04:03:26.560] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004985)

**[04:03:28.300] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-004986)

**[04:03:39.820] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004989)

**[04:03:58.300] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004995)

**[04:04:01.320] DepEd Undersecretary Carmela C. Oracion** (asr-004996)

**[04:05:17.980] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005025)

**[04:05:49.560] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005032)

**[04:05:58.180] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005034)

**[04:06:00.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005035)

**[04:06:06.360] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005037)

**[04:06:11.040] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005039)

**[04:06:14.220] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005040)

**[04:06:22.680] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005041)

**[04:06:27.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005042)

**[04:06:38.600] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005046)

**[04:06:44.320] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005048)

**[04:06:51.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005050)

**[04:06:57.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005052)  
To continue the aral program.

**[04:37:15.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005811)  
yeah, less than 2%.

**[04:37:18.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005812)  
So, and again, this is not for this year's,

**[04:37:20.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005813)  
not for right now,

**[04:37:22.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005814)  
but maybe for the plenary.

**[04:37:24.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005815)  
If you can help us understand,

**[04:37:26.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005816)  
ilan ba talaga yung mga bata

**[04:37:28.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005817)  
na nangangailangan ng special care

**[04:37:30.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005818)  
and special education,

**[04:37:32.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005819)  
that will be a very important starting point

**[04:37:34.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005820)  
kasi from there,

**[04:37:35.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005821)  
pwede natin matrack yung pangangailangan nila

**[04:37:38.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005822)  
across the system, no?

**[04:37:41.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005823)  
Sa ILRC, it's funded,

**[04:37:44.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005824)  
it's legislated,

**[04:37:46.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005825)  
but what more can we do?

**[04:37:48.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005826)  
Yung diagnosis, I know,

**[04:37:49.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005827)  
is a current issue

**[04:37:51.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005828)

**[04:37:53.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005829)  
who can diagnose

**[04:37:54.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005830)  
at alam ko mamahalin yung diagnosis.

**[04:37:57.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005831)  
That's one.

**[04:37:58.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005832)  
And then yung teachers, of course,

**[04:38:00.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005833)  
I think we talked about this earlier

**[04:38:02.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005834)  
with other senators,

**[04:38:04.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005835)  
but in the case na hindi mo kayang

**[04:38:06.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005836)  
makomplement ng lahat ng klase ng teachers,

**[04:38:11.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005837)  
do we have SNED teachers,

**[04:38:14.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005838)  
at least the basic SNED teachers

**[04:38:17.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005839)  
for these facilities?

**[04:38:18.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005840)  
Asec?

**[04:38:23.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005841)  
At this time po,

**[04:38:24.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005842)  
we only have in our data

**[04:38:25.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005843)  
6,000 plus SNED teachers

**[04:38:27.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005844)  
na under that plantelia,

**[04:38:29.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005845)  
specific plantelia.

**[04:38:30.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005846)  
Pero meron po tayong undergoing training

**[04:38:32.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005847)  
on receiving teachers.

**[04:38:34.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005848)  
In the inclusive education,

**[04:38:35.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005849)  
ang trend po ay kahit general education practitioner

**[04:38:39.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005850)  
would be able to cater to special needs.

**[04:38:41.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005851)  
With a little training,

**[04:38:42.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005852)  
with a little bit more training, no?

**[04:38:44.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005853)  
Okay, so dun sa 400,000,

**[04:38:48.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005854)  
suspected or we're assuming

**[04:38:51.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005855)  
na they're special needs children,

**[04:38:53.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005856)  
ilang guro yung kailangan nila?

**[04:38:56.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005857)  
Yung total na kailangan nilang guro?

**[04:38:58.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005858)  
Sa inclusive education principle po,

**[04:39:00.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005859)  
based on our SNED forum conducted two weeks ago,

**[04:39:04.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005860)  
ang tinitignan po natin ay sa mainstreaming

**[04:39:06.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005861)  
when we include our special learners,

**[04:39:09.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005862)  
at least we have one receiving teacher

**[04:39:11.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005863)  
for every five, ideally.

**[04:39:14.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005864)  
So that's 80,000 teachers?

**[04:39:17.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005865)  
Kung 400,000?

**[04:39:19.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005866)  
Most likely.

**[04:39:20.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005867)  
Yes, and we only have 6,000 teachers right now.

**[04:39:22.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005868)  
Yeah.

**[04:39:23.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005869)  
Okay.

**[04:39:24.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005870)  
But just to clarify,

**[04:39:26.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005871)  
na we don't really need the specialized teachers

**[04:39:29.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005872)  
because we will not have that one.

**[04:39:31.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005873)  
So we need to train our teachers

**[04:39:34.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005874)  
to be receiving teachers

**[04:39:36.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005875)  
for the inclusive education.

**[04:39:37.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005876)  
Okay.

**[04:39:38.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005877)  
So roughly,

**[04:39:39.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005878)  
whether they're formally trained

**[04:39:41.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005879)  
or they're a receiving teacher,

**[04:39:42.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005880)  
we need about 80,000 of them.

**[04:39:43.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005881)  
Correct, no?

**[04:39:44.120] DepEd Assistant Secretary Romeo T. Benviaje** (asr-005882)  
Opo.

**[04:39:45.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005883)  
To, to fulfill the ratio of

**[04:39:46.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005884)  
one is to five.

**[04:39:48.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005885)  
Well, ang ratio natin in Baguio, for example,

**[04:39:51.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005886)  
is one is to 3,700.

**[04:39:54.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005887)  
Ang ratio in Malabon is one is to 3,000.

**[04:39:57.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005888)  
Tarlac is doing better at one is to 1,200.

**[04:40:00.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005889)  
So one is to five is a huge jump from where we are.

**[04:40:04.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005890)  
But we're committed, ASEC, no?

**[04:40:07.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005891)  
We're committed to support this.

**[04:40:09.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005892)  
Anyway, there's a separate legislation for this.

**[04:40:11.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005893)  
So it's not like we have a choice.

**[04:40:13.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005894)  
We really have to push it.

**[04:40:14.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005895)  
At sabi nga ho namin,

**[04:40:15.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005896)  
yung mga special needs ng mga kabataan,

**[04:40:17.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005897)  
kailangan talaga ng special na tulong.

**[04:40:20.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005898)  
And we need to provide more resources for them, no?

**[04:40:24.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005899)  
So request ko po for the plenary.

**[04:40:27.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005900)  
Number one, help us understand the numbers.

**[04:40:29.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005901)  
Kasi yung 2% to 20% ang laki ng gap.

**[04:40:33.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005902)  
Ang laki ng jump na yan.

**[04:40:34.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005903)  
And I understand that 20% is an unverified study.

**[04:40:37.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005904)  
But you would be in a good position to tell us,

**[04:40:40.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005905)  
ilan ba talaga yan?

**[04:40:41.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005906)  
Huwag muna natin bilangin yung diagnosed

**[04:40:43.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005907)  
because alam natin na maraming

**[04:40:45.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005908)  
left undiagnosed.

**[04:40:46.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005909)  
So even yung sa tingin natin,

**[04:40:48.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005910)  
baka may pangailangang special,

**[04:40:50.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005911)  
i-note nyo na rin.

**[04:40:52.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005912)  
Tell us before the plenary kung ilan yan.

**[04:40:54.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005913)  
And then tell us the ratio.

**[04:40:56.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005914)  
Do I take it correctly na more than the facilities,

**[04:41:00.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005915)  
more than the paraphernalia and the equipment,

**[04:41:03.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005916)  
it's really the teachers that we need to focus on?

**[04:41:08.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005917)  
It's the teachers, Mr. President.

**[04:41:10.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005918)  
But it's also the access to development pediatricians

**[04:41:12.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005919)  
because it's a development pediatrician

**[04:41:14.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005920)  
who can accurately diagnose the learning deficiency

**[04:41:18.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005921)  
and prescribe whether that child is best included

**[04:41:21.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005922)  
with the larger class or best left to a one-to-one

**[04:41:24.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005923)  
or one-is-to-two special class.

**[04:41:27.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005924)  
We've had discussions about this.

**[04:41:29.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005925)  
Yun ang talagang, that intervention is missing.

**[04:41:31.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005926)  
So I said earlier, what's promising is what,

**[04:41:34.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005927)  
I know Dr. Yi is talking to Dr. Ho of PhilHealth.

**[04:41:38.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005928)  
So what they're doing and talking to the medical association

**[04:41:42.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005929)  
is very promising.

**[04:41:43.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005930)  
I think that one is an immediate solution

**[04:41:45.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005931)  
rather than trying to reach for nirvana

**[04:41:49.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005932)  
under the ILRC law which is probably decades away

**[04:41:53.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005933)  
given the budgetary requirements.

**[04:41:56.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005934)  
That's something that we can do tomorrow or next week.

**[04:41:59.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005935)  
Secretary, before I give the floor to Senator Joel,

**[04:42:03.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005936)  
yes, we will have the funding,

**[04:42:05.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005937)  
but we don't have enough of the developmental pediatricians

**[04:42:10.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005938)  
nor their time.

**[04:42:11.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005939)  
We don't have enough in the country, Mr. President.

**[04:42:12.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005940)  
Exactly.

**[04:42:13.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005941)  
So kahit may pondo tayo sa PhilHealth,

**[04:42:15.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005942)  
If you try to make an appointment for your child,

**[04:42:17.120] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005943)  
it will take you at least a year to get an appointment.

**[04:42:19.120] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005944)  
Let me ask Senator Joel who has personal experience here.

**[04:42:22.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005945)  
I was about to butt in, Mr. Chairman,

**[04:42:25.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005946)  
because I'm a father of a special kid, Mr. Chairman,

**[04:42:33.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005947)  
and it pains a lot because last time I visited Minuyan Elementary School

**[04:42:41.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005948)  
in San Jose del Monte,

**[04:42:43.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005949)  
and I actually immediately called the attention

**[04:42:48.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005950)  
of our good secretary about this

**[04:42:50.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005951)  
because when I went to this particular elementary school,

**[04:42:54.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005952)  
there are about 140, 150 students with special needs,

**[04:43:01.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005953)  
and I asked them how many teachers do they have.

**[04:43:05.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005954)  
They only have two.

**[04:43:06.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005955)  
Actually, they only have two.

**[04:43:08.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005956)  
And I asked them if the students were ready,

**[04:43:11.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005957)  
if they were already diagnosed.

**[04:43:13.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005958)  
And according to her, less than 10 students were diagnosed.

**[04:43:19.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005959)  
So this is really alarming,

**[04:43:21.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005960)  
and as mentioned by Secretary Angara,

**[04:43:26.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005961)  
the biggest problem right now is the development of the pediatrician.

**[04:43:30.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005962)  
I personally experienced that when you go to a development pediatrician,

**[04:43:35.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005963)  
in my case, I remember there are only two, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:43:40.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005964)  
There are only two.

**[04:43:41.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005965)  
One is Dr. Dimalanta of St. Luke's and Dr. Lexi Reyes of Makati Med.

**[04:43:47.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005966)  
And you're not even talking about occupational therapy, speech therapies, etc.

**[04:43:53.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005967)  
And so even in our discussions with the Committee on Higher and Technical Education,

**[04:43:59.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005968)  
we discussed this fully last time during the budget deliberation

**[04:44:03.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005969)  
when we were talking about the Dr. Parasabayan,

**[04:44:06.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005970)  
the implementation of Dr. Parasabayan,

**[04:44:08.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005971)  
which we are in a very good position.

**[04:44:10.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005972)  
In a very good position right now.

**[04:44:12.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005973)  
Before, there are only seven regions na meron pong public school of medicine.

**[04:44:20.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005974)  
Now there are, I mean, all regions have their own.

**[04:44:25.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005975)  
What's the number?

**[04:44:27.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005976)  
From eight state college universities offering school of medicine,

**[04:44:34.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005977)  
now it's 30.

**[04:44:35.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005978)  
Three zero.

**[04:44:37.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005979)  
But when we check as to how many development pediatricians are we producing,

**[04:44:43.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005980)  
and we asked the DOH and through the help of EDCOM,

**[04:44:48.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005981)  
dalawa lang daw po yung binibigay a year.

**[04:44:51.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005982)  
And so we were surprised kahit na nag-a-ask sila ng request for several years,

**[04:44:58.120] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005983)  
talagang ang maximum na binibigay.

**[04:45:00.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005984)  
Dalawa. Dalawang doktor, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:45:03.420] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005985)  
So, it's really a big problem.

**[04:45:05.660] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005986)  
And when you're talking about the 20%, Sir Yusek, with all due respect,

**[04:45:09.760] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005987)  
I'm not sure of the numbers, how we can actually double-check these figures.

**[04:45:16.480] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005988)  
Because there's a lot. There's a lot.

**[04:45:18.900] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005989)  
So, I hope that we can do something about this.

**[04:45:21.460] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005990)  
This is really personal to me, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:45:24.580] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005991)  
I hope we can do something.

**[04:45:26.380] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005992)  
But we can start with the teachers, of course.

**[04:45:28.820] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-005993)  
Because, again, there are so many schools out there na wala rin guru yung SNED at SPED nila.

**[04:45:35.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-005994)  
Thank you, Senator. Yes, Secretary.

**[04:45:37.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005995)  
With your permission, on the permission of Senator Joel and the Senate President.

**[04:45:41.920] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005996)  
The same way we passed the Dr. Parsabay, and I was still in the Senate when we passed that,

**[04:45:46.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005997)  
there was talk of passing a law, similar law, for allied health professionals, Your Honor.

**[04:45:53.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005998)  
Senator Joel, Senator Pia, I remember we were talking about that.

**[04:45:56.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-005999)  
And I think that's worth pursuing, Your Honor.

**[04:45:58.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006000)  
And maybe look at the School of Health Sciences model of PGH, where the problem is geographic.

**[04:46:07.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006001)  
There are many areas that have no takers, no offerers of these courses.

**[04:46:11.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006002)  
So, you really have no pipeline down the road, Mr. President.

**[04:46:16.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006003)  
But if you have, similar to the School of Health Sciences of PGH in Tacloban, in Coronadal,

**[04:46:22.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006004)  
which are not quite getting the attention, but they're, kasi may return service huyon eh.

**[04:46:28.600] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006005)  
So, you have your STs or speech therapists, your OTs, occupational therapists.

**[04:46:33.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006006)  
You will have them with the requirement to serve for four years.

**[04:46:36.180] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006007)  
Maaring ma-pirate yon, but they have to serve their four years, Mr. President.

**[04:46:39.920] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006008)  
And then, you just produce a consistent pipeline.

**[04:46:42.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006009)  
So, it would help to have, if not a law, then a program for allied health professionals, Your Honor.

**[04:46:48.060] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006010)  
Thank you, Secretaries.

**[04:46:49.480] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006011)  
Maybe just to end this point, we know that yung Sned or yung SPED, it used to be called SPED.

**[04:46:58.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006012)  
Yung Sned na yung pangalan.

**[04:47:00.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006013)  
It's something we've been doing, but we all see that meron talagang gaps.

**[04:47:06.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006014)  
Whether it's a personal gap, a funding gap.

**[04:47:11.240] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006015)  
And I'm hoping that while I'm chairman here, mahanapan natin ito ng solusyon.

**[04:47:17.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006016)  
And it's not as easy as just putting money.

**[04:47:20.900] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006017)  
Kasi we know that that solves some problems, but not all problems.

**[04:47:25.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006018)  
So, we have actually asked EDCOM to take a further...

**[04:47:28.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006019)  
look into the way that we provide education for our special needs children.

**[04:47:36.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006020)  
And maybe in preparation for next year's budget or next year's implementation,

**[04:47:41.900] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006021)  
or next next year's budget or implementation, we can take a more detailed look at the numbers.

**[04:47:48.980] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006022)  
Kasi currently, EDCOM 2 says 20%.

**[04:47:51.300] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006023)  
You're saying it's 2%.

**[04:47:52.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006024)  
So, dun pa lang, di na tayo nagkaka-agree, di ba?

**[04:47:55.280] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006025)  
So, we can look at that.

**[04:47:56.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006026)  
We can look at the pipeline.

**[04:47:58.500] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006027)  
We can look at the pipeline of teachers, and EDCOM naman also includes CHED and even TESDA.

**[04:48:02.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006028)  
So, we can look at how we can try to affect that pipeline as well.

**[04:48:07.060] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006029)  
Then at the proper time, pag panahon na talaga na mapondohan natin ito ng mas maganda at mas maayos,

**[04:48:13.220] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006030)  
kapag malinaw na yung magandang gawin, then I think we can come together for this.

**[04:48:17.580] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006031)  
Secretary.

**[04:48:18.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006032)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[04:48:19.780] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006033)  
We really appreciate that concern for special needs education.

**[04:48:24.660] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006034)  
But perhaps we should also be looking at it alongside universities.

**[04:48:28.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006035)  
So, I think that's the impetus really for really all institutions, all possible institutions coming together.

**[04:48:43.520] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006036)  
Because if DepEd tries to address it in a DepEd fashion with DepEd funding alone, then it will take many decades.

**[04:48:50.000] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006037)  
But if all institutions come together to produce the services, it's really a service that's needed.

**[04:48:57.300] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006038)  
An accurate diagnosis.

**[04:48:58.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006039)  
So, the diagnosis doesn't have to come from a DepEd doctor.

**[04:49:01.620] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006040)  
It doesn't have to come, it can come from any doctor.

**[04:49:04.760] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006041)  
It just needs someone to pay for that service down the road.

**[04:49:07.620] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006042)  
And that public school student definitely does not have the capacity to pay.

**[04:49:10.980] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006043)  
But because we subscribe to universal healthcare, then there should be a package for these children.

**[04:49:17.500] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006044)  
Because I think that's the spirit of field.

**[04:49:19.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006045)  
It's to care for the ones who need it the most, Mr. Chair.

**[04:49:22.580] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006046)  
Yes, I agree.

**[04:49:23.640] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006047)  
Secretary.

**[04:49:23.860] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006048)  
May I just, just very short time, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:49:26.060] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006049)  
I agree with what the Secretary mentioned.

**[04:49:28.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006050)  
The whole of government approach.

**[04:49:30.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006051)  
Unfortunately, for the past so many years, parang walang nag-aasikaso, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:49:35.180] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006052)  
There are low-hanging fruits that we can actually implement right away.

**[04:49:41.000] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006053)  
For example, PCMC, in a year, five lang yung scholars nila, natitrain nila.

**[04:49:45.800] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006054)  
But they can do 15, at least 15.

**[04:49:48.680] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006055)  
NCH, National Children's, eight lang a year.

**[04:49:52.220] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006056)  
PGH, two a year, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:49:54.300] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006057)  
They can do plus 10 at least, plus 10 for NCH.

**[04:49:58.500] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006058)  
But there are only 35 plantilia that we are looking at, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:50:02.720] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006059)  
These are things that we just discovered that we can actually do right away without going through the mills of passing laws, etc.

**[04:50:13.720] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006060)  
So, again, Mr. Chairman, thank you for this opportunity that we were able to tackle this very important topic.

**[04:50:20.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006061)  
Thank you.

**[04:50:21.020] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006062)  
And, Secretary, three of the five EDCOM members are here, so we will ask EDCOM to take a special look at this, no?

**[04:50:28.020] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006063)  
And include PhilHealth, kasi kapag yung PhilHealth, bukas na yung pondo sa kanila,

**[04:50:32.820] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006064)  
it might even generate more individuals going into these fields which can specifically cater to the special needs.

**[04:50:39.260] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006065)  
We agree with you wholeheartedly. Thank you very much, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:50:41.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006066)  
Yes, thank you. Thank you for that.

**[04:50:43.000] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006067)  
Okay, I have, unless the Senate President has some more points, I have a few more points.

**[04:50:47.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006068)  
Go ahead.

**[04:50:47.860] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006069)  
Go ahead, okay.

**[04:50:48.880] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006070)  
El Nino, okay.

**[04:50:51.580] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006071)  
We're assuming that yung tubig mas magiging mahirap in a few months.

**[04:50:58.080] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006072)  
I know there are already a hundred plus municipalities who have declared an emergency because of El Nino.

**[04:51:05.700] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006073)  
Are our schools prepared for a possible shortage of water?

**[04:51:11.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006074)  
Is that something we're looking at, Secretary?

**[04:51:13.680] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006075)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:51:15.380] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006076)  
We're looking at rising temperatures, so we want each school, ideally, or each classroom, actually,

**[04:51:21.820] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006077)  
to have a, what they call a jerry can, yung lalagyan ng tubig.

**[04:51:28.020] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006078)  
For, so to prevent dehydration, Your Honor.

**[04:51:30.840] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006079)  
And of course, in the extreme situations, merong pag tumaman na po ng 40 plus degrees yung temperature,

**[04:51:37.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006080)  
then you have to send, really have to send, even before that, you have to send kids home already.

**[04:51:41.880] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006081)  
Because you'll have headaches, their noses will start bleeding, et cetera.

**[04:51:47.100] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006082)  
So, to be clear, kapag high temperature, you're going to call off classes?

**[04:51:52.460] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006083)  
We may have to, Your Honor.

**[04:51:53.420] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006084)  
Is that done at the school level or at the LGU level?

**[04:51:55.720] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006085)  
Well, because that's an emergency.

**[04:51:58.020] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006086)  
The school is actually empowered to do that under existing EOs if it reaches emergency levels already, Your Honor.

**[04:52:04.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006087)  
But generally, it's the LGU who would call off classes, Your Honor.

**[04:52:09.800] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006088)  
And, well, gusto ko lang i-flag lang yung lack of water for the CR, for the canteen, drinking water, for hydration.

**[04:52:19.680] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006089)  
Naka-budget na ba tong El Nino preparation natin for the schools?

**[04:52:23.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006090)  
That's a lot of, a lot of, ano ba yan?

**[04:52:28.020] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006091)  
Mga jerry cans o mga...

**[04:52:29.920] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006092)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:52:30.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006093)  
Yung rainwater catchment systems, et cetera.

**[04:52:35.640] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006094)  
We will have to include it in their school MOU, Your Honor.

**[04:52:38.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006095)  
Okay.

**[04:52:39.040] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006096)  
So, this next year's MOU, kasama tong mga provisions na ito?

**[04:52:46.320] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006097)  
O ipagkakasya na lang nila?

**[04:52:47.640] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006098)  
Parang basically...

**[04:52:48.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006099)  
Yeah, they'll have to work with what we have, Your Honor, to fund this.

**[04:52:54.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006100)  
Kasi kasama na rin sa'n yung hinihingi nating additional security.

**[04:52:58.020] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006101)  
Guards, et cetera.

**[04:52:59.420] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006102)  
So, these are all things which eat up the MOU.

**[04:53:03.240] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006103)  
And then, we're also looking at maybe some upgrading the power systems of schools down the road.

**[04:53:09.560] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006104)  
Because pag mainit, baka pwede nga ang solar power na lang sila.

**[04:53:12.680] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006105)  
Of course, that's a very long-term solution.

**[04:53:14.940] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006106)  
For the lights and the fans, no?

**[04:53:16.200] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006107)  
I think kaya yung lights and electric fans ng solar, no?

**[04:53:20.000] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006108)  
Okay.

**[04:53:20.620] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006109)  
So, Secretary, maybe just as a suggestion, not really a budget item, but going into next year,

**[04:53:28.020] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006110)  
maybe a memo from the executive talking to the teachers and the principals on how to prepare for the El Nino.

**[04:53:35.320] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006111)  
Yes, we'll do that.

**[04:53:36.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006112)  
Catchment of water, mga balde, mga jerrycan, or ways to be able to keep water for sanitation.

**[04:53:44.700] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006113)  
Iba pa yung water for drinking, no?

**[04:53:46.940] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006114)  
For drinking, yes, Your Honor.

**[04:53:47.600] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006115)  
Oo.

**[04:53:48.400] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006116)  
Kasi kung bibili yung mga skwelahan ng drinking water, that's also going to be a cost in the MOUE.

**[04:53:56.220] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006117)  
That's right, Your Honor.

**[04:53:57.020] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006118)  
Yeah.

**[04:53:58.020] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006119)  
Please take a look at that.

**[04:53:58.900] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006120)  
Will do, Your Honor.

**[04:54:00.540] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006121)  
We have questions, SP, or shall I finish my questions, na?

**[04:54:05.660] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006122)  
Sige, Senator Joe, you first.

**[04:54:07.460] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006123)  
You first, you first.

**[04:54:10.560] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006124)  
Okay.

**[04:54:11.980] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006125)  
I have just a few questions, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:54:15.600] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006126)  
In fact, I will forego with probably more than half or 70% of what I prepared for today.

**[04:54:24.200] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006127)  
I'm happy that a lot of our...

**[04:54:28.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006128)  
concerns were already tackled, but probably the most pressing would be the budget for ARAL program, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:54:36.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006129)  
I hope that we can really do something about it.

**[04:54:38.920] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006130)  
The request kanina is 8 billion, Mr. Chairman, so we'll see.

**[04:54:45.860] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006131)  
We'll see what we can do.

**[04:54:46.940] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006132)  
I hope we can help each other out.

**[04:54:49.680] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006133)  
Mr. Chairman, Secretary, I am sure you have known me for 100 years now, and I have been raising this issue of jobs.

**[04:54:58.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006134)  
Job orders and contract of service.

**[04:55:02.240] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006135)  
We wanted to find out yung significant number of JO, COS personnel in DepEd's workforce.

**[04:55:11.040] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006136)  
Nitignan ko naman, nag-i-improve, pero Secretary, you have 1,088,991 authorized position this year.

**[04:55:22.460] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006137)  
Ang unfilled po is still 45,000, no?

**[04:55:28.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006138)  
And you have 44,000 job orders and a contract of service.

**[04:55:33.340] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006139)  
Yung unfilled na 45,000, galing po yan sa 48,711.

**[04:55:39.460] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006140)  
So we just wanted to point that out because wala naman akong pinapalampas na department at agency dito sa Senado because this has been my advocacy.

**[04:55:50.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006141)  
So again, paulit-ulit yung questions ko dito.

**[04:55:55.160] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006142)  
Functions they perform?

**[04:55:58.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006143)  
Political plantilla vacancies to be prioritized?

**[04:56:04.140] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006144)  
To reduce yung reliance natin dito sa contractual personnel for continuing functions?

**[04:56:11.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006145)  
May we just get an answer, Mr. Chairman?

**[04:56:14.360] DepEd Secretary Sonny Angara** (asr-006146)  
Yes, Your Honor, I'm witness to your consistency in your advocacy, but with your permission, can I turn it over to our head of HR, Usec Willie?

**[04:56:22.640] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006147)  
Yes, Usec Willie, please.

**[04:56:23.960] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006148)  
Yes, Mr. Chair, thank you very much.

**[04:56:27.440] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006149)  
Actually,

**[04:56:28.020] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006150)  
we have that 45,000 unfilled, but we have done a lot of audit and catching up in the filling up.

**[04:56:37.440] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006151)  
But unahin ko lang po yung job orders nationwide kasi while we have that number, we offer them to a plantilla position given the vacancies and unfilled.

**[04:56:53.360] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006152)  
However, since a requirement is eligibility,

**[04:56:56.600] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006153)  
hindi po rin namin,

**[04:56:58.020] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006154)  
sila makuha.

**[04:56:59.160] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006155)  
What we're doing in the department is we regularly conduct review sessions and helping them out in taking the career service examination,

**[04:57:09.740] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006156)  
but to no avail.

**[04:57:11.100] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006157)  
But we don't have opportunity for them to be absorbed.

**[04:57:16.620] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006158)  
Nonetheless, we maintain them rather than mawalang po sila ng trabaho.

**[04:57:20.580] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006159)  
That's one of the many reasons.

**[04:57:22.520] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006160)  
Quick question, Usec.

**[04:57:23.960] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006161)  
Dun sa unfilled positions, talagang...

**[04:57:26.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006162)  
Kasi there are...

**[04:57:28.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006163)  
agencies, departments, na minsan talagang hindi nila mapi-fill up yung position na nandun.

**[04:57:32.680] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006164)  
So, kailangan nilang gumawa ng parahan.

**[04:57:35.320] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006165)  
Another example is, for example, yung sa COA.

**[04:57:39.260] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006166)  
Mga auditors naman, wala naman takers doon sa kaya gusto naman nila ngayon na salary exemption, rationalization, etc.

**[04:57:50.280] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006167)  
Dito po, ano yung pinaka...

**[04:57:53.360] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006168)  
Because I wanted to help.

**[04:57:54.380] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006169)  
I'm asking these questions because I wanted to help.

**[04:57:57.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006170)  
Because lumaki pa po.

**[04:57:58.020] Senator Joel Villanueva** (asr-006171)  
So, yung job order po ninyo from 37,000, now it's 44,000.

**[04:58:02.820] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006172)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[04:58:04.280] DepEd Undersecretary Willy Cabral** (asr-006173)  
Una, meron po kami yung mga tinatawag na hard-to-fill positions.

**[05:11:07.720] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006474)  
Um, so I'd like to give just a little bit of time for Mr. Deloy just to talk about what they've been doing with DepEd, um, with regard to school safety and trying to address the emerging concerns that we have.

**[05:11:19.560] Senator Paolo Benigno "Bam" Aquino IV** (asr-006475)  
Just briefly, ah, for Stairway Foundation.

**[05:11:23.500] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006476)  
Maraming salamat po.

**[05:11:24.500] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006477)  
So, ah, Mr., Mr. Chair.

**[05:11:26.640] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006478)  
So, ang Stairway Foundation po, we've been working with DepEd since 2012.

**[05:11:30.360] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006479)  
So, we've seen the evolution of the challenges in child protection and education.

**[05:11:34.720] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006480)  
Um, I would say that this, at this time, ito yung pinaka-challenging talaga within the education sector in relation to child protection.

**[05:11:43.020] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006481)  
And we appreciate yung framing ng School Safety Act because we believe that violence doesn't exist in a vacuum.

**[05:11:50.500] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006482)  
Maraming interchanging forces that influences violence.

**[05:11:53.940] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006483)  
So, when we started working with DepEd, we've explored different modalities in terms of educating learners and training teachers to build up their capacity on violence prevention.

**[05:12:06.460] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006484)  
Ah, next slide, please.

**[05:12:09.320] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006485)  
So, yung isang breakthrough po, actually, happened in 2024.

**[05:12:14.520] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006486)  
Ito yung tinatawag natin na anti-OSAEC classroom-based sessions, wherein the challenge was how do you now, how do you do prevention and education sessions directly inside the classroom?

**[05:12:28.560] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006489)  
Ito po ay enabled ng implementing rules and regulation ng RA 11930.

**[05:12:33.820] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006490)  
So, actually, may mandate si DepEd na dapat inside the classroom, nagtuturo ng mga gantong concepts tulad ng body boundaries, consent, recognizing risky and unsafe situations, and help-seeking behavior.

**[05:12:45.800] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006491)  
The good thing is that noong in-implementation ng 2024, since 2024, at tuloy-tuloy, the DepEd, through the...

**[05:12:53.920] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006492)  
The DepEd Learners' Rights and Protection Division has managed to reach 8.5 million learners.

**[05:13:00.540] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006493)  
And sa isang outcome study, tatlo sa apat na learners, yung nagsabi na nag-increase yung self-protection knowledge nila and yung willingness to disclose.

**[05:13:10.340] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006494)  
In fact, hindi ko sinama sa slide, doon sa last tranche of implementation, nagkaroon ng 4,000 disclosures after the session.

**[05:13:17.860] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006495)  
So imagine that, 4,000 learners who

**[05:13:23.920] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006496)  
have received the intervention as mandated by the DepEd protocols on helping learners.

**[05:13:29.540] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006497)  
4,000 na maaring hindi nag-disclose kung walang gantong targeted session.

**[05:13:34.600] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006498)  
So, it is a very successful program, I would say, na ginawa ng DepEd Learner Rights and Protection Division.

**[05:13:42.140] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006499)  
Next slide po.

**[05:13:44.640] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006500)  
So, yung issue natin na isa ay bullying.

**[05:13:49.160] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006501)  
So, looking at the model of the anti-OSAEC classroom-based education,

**[05:13:53.920] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006502)  
Again, together with the Learner Rights and Protection Division, we've developed anti-bullying classroom-based sessions.

**[05:14:01.280] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006503)  
So, ang kagandahan, it was developed in consultation with learners and personnel themselves.

**[05:14:08.260] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006504)  
So, siya po ay kade-develop lang this year and hopefully it will be deployed in time for the National Children's Month of 2026.

**[05:14:16.760] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006505)  
Next slide po.

**[05:14:17.340] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006506)  
So, min-mirror niya yung same strategy at yun nga, tulad ng nabang ito, very consultative yung development.

**[05:14:27.880] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006507)  
Next slide po.

**[05:14:31.040] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006508)  
Next.

**[05:14:33.940] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006509)  
Nag-de-develop din po kami with the Learner Rights and Protection Division ng isang companion app, learning app,

**[05:14:39.180] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006510)  
na pwedeng i-leverage yung technology to teach bullying prevention knowledge to children.

**[05:14:47.340] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006511)  
Next po.

**[05:14:49.700] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006512)  
Tapos nakikita natin kanina yung power ng peer-to-peer approach.

**[05:14:54.340] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006513)  
So, the way Stairway works with DepEd is that we take our local implementation experience, which...

**[05:15:00.000] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006514)  
is evidence-based, and then scale it up with DepEd

**[05:15:03.080] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006515)  
para mas makarating sa mas maraming learners.

**[05:15:05.860] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006516)  
Kasi if we are looking at these various child protection problems

**[05:15:09.300] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006517)  
as a national problem, the solution should also be national in scope.

**[05:15:13.940] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006518)  
Marami kasing schools, yung piecemeal,

**[05:15:16.080] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006519)  
nag-implement na sarili nilang intervention,

**[05:15:18.180] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006520)  
pero marami naging schools yung merong gaps in capacity

**[05:15:22.620] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006521)  
in terms of anong materials yung gagamitin ko.

**[05:15:25.640] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006522)  
And this is where these interventions come in.

**[05:15:27.840] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006523)  
So for example, this peer educators program na ginagawa namin,

**[05:15:32.240] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006524)  
next slide po,

**[05:15:34.640] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006525)  
it's being implemented in our home base in Puerto Galera

**[05:15:37.740] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006526)  
and discusses these different topics wherein, next slide,

**[05:15:42.880] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006527)  
ang nagde-deliver ay mga peer educators themselves.

**[05:15:45.660] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006528)  
So nakita namin yung kapangyarihan na kapag yung message ay nanggagaling sa kapwa bata

**[05:15:50.300] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006529)  
at naka-design safely yung materials,

**[05:15:53.280] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006530)  
mas mataas yung learning retention in terms of these safety messages.

**[05:15:57.080] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006531)  
Next slide.

**[05:15:57.840] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006532)  
And now last but not the least,

**[05:16:00.780] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006533)  
yung isang magandang framing sa child protection program ng DEPED is,

**[05:16:05.880] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006534)  
yun nga, yung nabanggit ng sekretary kanina,

**[05:16:08.280] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006535)  
yung kaagapay program was originally for academic nature,

**[05:16:13.640] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006536)  
pero dahil nga sa nangyaring spate of school violence,

**[05:16:18.200] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006537)  
na-transform siya to be more of a, dun sa child protection yung framing.

**[05:16:23.480] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006538)  
So ang ginawa po namin with DEPED is to develop what we call

**[05:16:27.680] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006539)  
the Kaagapay Parenting in the Digital Age,

**[05:16:30.520] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006540)  
kung saan nare-recognize natin na marami doon sa mga violence cases,

**[05:16:36.860] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006541)  
na-disrupt sana natin kung nagkaroon ng early detection,

**[05:16:40.260] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006542)  
kung si parent na-detect na niya, teka lang may pinagdadaan yung anak ko,

**[05:16:44.680] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006543)  
and nag-access siya ng help sa school, for example, sa advisor, sa school guidance counselor,

**[05:16:51.520] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006544)  
na may napapansin po ka sana ako, baka pwedeng pagtulungan natin.

**[05:16:56.960] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006545)  
So what?

**[05:16:57.680] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006546)  
So what we did was we developed a very simple session guide and session deck,

**[05:17:01.940] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006547)  
tapos nagkaroon ngayon ng series of rollouts sa regional levels at magkakaroon ng cascading training.

**[05:17:07.660] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006548)  
In fact, nagkaroon ng dalawang Kaagapay Parenting Roadshow sa Cainta Rizal and Santiago Isabela,

**[05:17:15.000] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006549)  
at napakaganda ng feedback ng parents.

**[05:17:17.180] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006550)  
So ano po yung tinuturo sa Kaagapay?

**[05:17:19.380] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006551)  
Number one, yung tinatawag namin na four C's of online risks.

**[05:17:23.800] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006552)  
Mahirap gabaya ng mga parents yung anak nila kung hindi nila naintindihan,

**[05:17:27.680] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006553)  
ano yung mga iba't-ibang online risks na maaaring kaharapin ng mga anak nila.

**[05:17:32.340] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006554)  
Pangalawa, ano yung mga online parenting and reporting tools, ano yung options nila?

**[05:17:36.940] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006555)  
At for us, yung pinaka-importante yung huli, yung parenting strategies.

**[05:17:41.480] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006556)  
Last slide, please.

**[05:17:44.060] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006557)  
So ito po yung, kumusta, kasama ka, sundo ka, Kaagapay?

**[05:17:47.000] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006558)  
Ito po yung framing.

**[05:17:48.340] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006559)  
So basically, ang tinuturo sa parents, next click po,

**[05:17:51.720] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006560)  
sa kumusta, pinapaunawa sa mga parents na importante yung pangumusta sa araw-araw na buhay,

**[05:17:57.680] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006561)  
na pag-uwi mo ng bahay, hindi mo lang kakamusta yung anak mo tungkol sa kamusta ang exam mo,

**[05:18:04.700] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006562)  
kamusta yung test, et cetera, kundi may nagpasaya ba sa'yo ngayong araw, may nagpalungkot ba sa'yo?

**[05:18:11.420] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006563)  
At yung paraan ng pagtatunong ay napaka-importante.

**[05:18:14.640] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006564)  
Pangalawa, yung kasama.

**[05:18:16.460] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006565)  
Kapag nagkapag-establish mo agad ng rapport,

**[05:18:19.360] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006566)  
kailangan maintindihan ng parents yung ginagalawa na online ng mundo ng anak.

**[05:18:23.060] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006567)  
So dito tinuturo sa parents, ano yung strategy na,

**[05:18:25.960] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006568)  
paano mo maintindihan yung kinagigiliwa ng anak mo na online games?

**[05:18:30.640] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006569)  
May isang parent during our session, nag-share siya at narinig na ibang parents.

**[05:18:35.100] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006570)  
Para maintindihan ko, nag-create ako ng Roblox account at kaming dalawa,

**[05:18:39.540] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006571)  
naglalaro kami ng anak ko magkasama.

**[05:18:41.780] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006572)  
So mas naintindihan ko ngayon.

**[05:18:44.240] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006573)  
Next po, yung kasundo.

**[05:18:46.760] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006574)  
Ito yung paglalatag ng online safety family agreements.

**[05:18:52.100] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006575)  
Importante sa mga pamilya na mayroong hangganan and boundaries.

**[05:18:55.960] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006576)  
In terms of digital usage, digital lives ng mga bata.

**[05:18:59.960] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006577)  
Ito yung structure na kailangan.

**[05:19:01.340] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006578)  
Halimbawa, pag sa hapagkainan, wala muna tayong gadget use.

**[05:19:06.640] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006579)  
Kaya lang, sinasabi rin namin sa mga parents, kung mag-i-employ tayo ng ganitong agreement,

**[05:19:11.120] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006580)  
dapat kayo rin bilang parent, huwag kayong gagamit ng cellphone habang nasa hapagkainan.

**[05:19:17.160] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006581)  
And last but not the least is yung kaagapay concept.

**[05:19:20.320] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006582)  
Na kung saan tinuturo namin sa mga parents na kapag may nag-disclose na anak,

**[05:19:25.200] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006583)  
dapat ganito yung paraan ng paghahandol ng disclosure.

**[05:19:29.520] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006584)  
Walang victim blaming.

**[05:19:31.820] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006585)  
Makikinig ka.

**[05:19:33.020] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006586)  
At very importantly, i-access mo yung support system sa paaralan.

**[05:19:37.880] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006587)  
So dito po yung framing noong kaagapay, parenting in the digital age.

**[05:19:42.580] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006588)  
So kung titignan nyo, ang concept ay parenting in the digital age, hindi digital parenting.

**[05:19:50.740] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006589)  
Kasi never mas magiging expert ang magulang sa anak sa technology.

**[05:19:55.200] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006590)  
Pero may mga parenting skills na kahit hindi teki yung magulang,

**[05:20:00.560] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006591)  
pag in-apply niya sa anak niya, mas magagabayin yung anak niya sa online world.

**[05:20:05.660] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006592)  
So yun po yun, Jis.

**[05:20:06.600] Stairway Foundation Child Protection Specialist Ysrael C. Diloy** (asr-006593)  
Pero ito po yung pinaka ilan sa intervention and partnerships na pinwede aside from others.
